*Liubov Chubarova,Alexandra Kuleshova,Daniil Volkov,Kirill Sultanov,Alexey Zaytsev*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出BEAR-Bench，一个包含1000个人工标注问题的英俄双语企业及学术文档推理基准，评估了16个MLLM并发现即使最强模型也有明显提升空间，同时比较了幻觉检测方法的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM基准过度强调信息抽取、依赖外部领域知识、仅将专业文档作为众多场景之一，且主要集中于英语和中文，导致俄语等语言严重缺乏代表性，无法全面评估模型在文本密集的专业文档推理能力。

Method: 构建BEAR-Bench基准：包含1000个基于商业和科学文档的人工标注问题，覆盖英语和俄语；评估16个专有和开源MLLM（包括Gemini 3.1 Pro和Qwen3.5-397B）；并利用模型输出对现有幻觉检测方法进行比较分析。

Result: 最强系统在BEAR-Bench上仍存在明显性能差距（headroom）；对幻觉检测方法的比较揭示了各方法在识别模型失败方面的可靠性差异。

Conclusion: BEAR-Bench填补了俄语专业文档推理基准的空白，展示了当前MLLM在处理文本密集双语专业文档时的不足，并为幻觉检测方法的选择提供了实证依据。

Abstract: While Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have made significant strides in visual comprehension, their ability to reason about text-dense, professional documents remains incompletely evaluated. Existing benchmarks emphasize information extraction, require external domain knowledge, or cover professional documents only as one of many settings. They are also largely English- or Chinese-centric, leaving other languages and Russian, in particular, substantially underrepresented. To address these limitations, we introduce BEAR-Bench (Bilingual Enterprise and Academic Reasoning), a self-contained, complex English-and-Russian benchmark comprising 1000 human-annotated questions based on text-rich business and scientific documents. We evaluate 16 proprietary and open-weight MLLMs, including Gemini 3.1 Pro and Qwen3.5-397B, on BEAR-Bench and observe clear headroom even for the strongest systems. Finally, we use the resulting model outputs to compare existing hallucination detection methods, evaluating not only how often models fail on BEAR-Bench but also how reliably those failures can be identified.

</details>


### [128] [CABLE: Extending the Reach of Memory Retrieval via Complementary Antecedent-Based Linking and Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17911)
*Zheling Tan,Jin Gao,Dequan Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CABLE方法，一种即插即用的增强模块，用于解决LLM智能体长期会话记忆中的"证据可达性"问题。CABLE不依赖语义相似性的记忆图链接，而是构建与宿主检索器互补的稀疏前因关联，通过生成前因导向查询、检索先行记忆、减去语义近邻候选并验证剩余项，在检索时沿这些链接扩展种子结果，以表面化隐含的支持性证据，在多个基准和LLM后端上均取得更高评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的长期会话记忆系统在检索时过度依赖语义相似性，虽然有利于主题性回忆，但往往遗漏了与后续事件语义距离较远但对其有解释作用的早期经验、计划或动机。现有记忆图的链接主要由语义重叠驱动，与宿主检索器的结果重复，无法真正扩展检索范围。

Method: CABLE作为插件式增强模块，对每条新记忆生成前因导向的查询，检索过往记忆，然后减去宿主检索器直接语义邻域中的候选，对剩余候选进行验证后将接受的互补关联加入稀疏有向图。在检索时，CABLE沿这些链接扩展宿主系统的检索种子，以发现隐含的支持性证据。

Result: CABLE在LoCoMo和MA-LongMemEval上使用A-MEM评估，并集成到SimpleMem和Mem0g中，结合Qwen3.5-27B、DeepSeek-chat和GPT-4o-mini，在所有评估的系统级设置中均获得更高的平均LLM评判分数。在开放域、多会话和偏好导向问题等证据跨记忆或会话分布的场景中增益最大。

Conclusion: 结果支持优先构建稀疏的、与推理相关的、能够补充而非重复宿主检索器的关联。这证明记忆图的核心价值不在于增加更多链接，而在于构建能够真正扩展检索器语义覆盖范围的互补性关联。

Abstract: As LLM agents operate across structured workflows and sessions, preserving long-term history does not ensure that later contexts can recover relevant evidence through a bounded memory interface. We study this evidence-reachability problem in long-term conversational memory, where retrieval still relies heavily on semantic similarity. This works well for topical recall, but it often misses earlier experiences, plans, or motivations that are semantically distant from the later events they help explain. Existing memory graphs provide cross-memory structure, yet links driven mainly by semantic overlap can duplicate what the host retriever already recovers. We argue that link construction should instead prioritize a sparse set of retriever-complementary associations. We present CABLE (Complementary Antecedent-Based Linking and Expansion), a plug-in augmentation that constructs links designed to extend the host retriever's direct semantic reach. For each new memory, CABLE generates antecedent-oriented queries, retrieves prior memories, subtracts candidates in the direct semantic neighborhood, and verifies the remainder before adding the accepted complementary associations into a sparse directed graph. At retrieval time, CABLE expands the host system's retrieved seeds along these links to surface implicit supporting evidence. We evaluate CABLE with A-MEM on LoCoMo and MA-LongMemEval, and further integrate it into SimpleMem and Mem0g on LoCoMo, using Qwen3.5-27B, DeepSeek-chat, and GPT-4o-mini. CABLE yields higher mean LLM-judge scores in every evaluated system-level setting, with the largest gains in categories where useful evidence is distributed across memories or sessions, including open-domain, multi-session, and preference-oriented questions. These results support prioritizing sparse, reasoning-relevant associations that complement rather than duplicate the host retriever.

</details>


### [129] [SpeechSense: A Paralinguistic-Focused Dataset for Fine-Grained Speech Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17931)
*Shicheng Ma,Wenqian Cui,Irwin King*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了SpeechSense数据集，用于细粒度语音情感分析。该数据集定义了8类人际立场（如自信、不耐烦等）的分类体系，这些立场主要依赖韵律线索而非词汇内容来识别。通过高保真语音合成和人工验证构建，实验证明具有声学信息访问的模型优于纯文本基线，验证了声学线索在检测微妙说话者态度中的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音情感分析研究存在两个主要局限：一是主流方法依赖级联ASR与文本分析的文本中心流程，会丢失韵律和语调等关键声学特征；二是现有基准存在标签粒度不匹配问题，优先考虑基本情绪而非社交敏感度所需的细微人际立场。

Method: 作者提出SpeechSense数据集，首先定义了8类人际立场的专用分类体系（主要通过韵律线索而非词汇内容识别），然后基于该分类体系构建数据集，使用高保真语音合成技术并经过严格人工验证。实验涵盖多模态LLM、纯文本LLM和语音编码器。

Result: 实验表明，具有声学信息访问的模型在检测微妙说话者态度方面始终优于纯文本基线模型，从而实证验证了声学线索在细粒度语音情感分析中的首要地位。

Conclusion: 研究结果强调了SpeechSense的必要性，证实了在语音情感分析中声学信息比文本内容更为关键，为未来细粒度语音情感分析研究提供了新的基准数据集。

Abstract: Recent advances in AI have revolutionized speech processing, yet effective speech understanding requires discerning not just what is said, but how it is said. Speech Sentiment Analysis plays a critical role in decoding these paralinguistic cues for diverse real-world applications such as recruitment and customer service. However, existing Speech Sentiment Analysis research faces two primary limitations. First, dominant approaches rely on text-centric pipelines that cascade Automatic Speech Recognition with text analysis. This process inevitably discards essential acoustic features like prosody and tone, failing to capture attitudinal meanings in acoustically ambiguous utterances. Second, current benchmarks suffer from a mismatch in label granularity, prioritizing basic emotions (e.g., happy, sad) over the nuanced interpersonal stances (e.g., confident, impatient) necessary for social sensitivity. To address these limitations, we propose a novel dataset, SpeechSense, for fine-grained speech sentiment analysis. Specifically, we define a specialized 8-class taxonomy of interpersonal stances detectable primarily through prosodic cues beyond lexical content alone. We then construct a curated dataset based on this taxonomy, built from high-fidelity speech synthesis and rigorous human validation. Comprehensive experiments across multi-modal LLMs, text-only LLMs, and speech encoders demonstrate that models with acoustic access consistently outperform text-only baselines. These results empirically validate the primacy of acoustic cues in detecting subtle speaker attitudes, highlighting the necessity of SpeechSense. Dataset and supplementary materials are available at https://github.com/Sher13cked/SpeechSense.

</details>


### [130] [Grading Needs a Rubric, Not Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17938)
*Jhen-Ke Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出any-to-bench框架，主张小型语言模型在依据明确评分标准(rubric)评分时，其可靠性可与昂贵的前沿模型媲美。该方法的核心设计是：前沿模型仅在数据摄入时读取源文档提取问题与评分标准，之后所有重复性评分工作均由低成本模型完成。实验分析了3,456个评分数据，发现分数主要由答案本身决定（方差贡献95.6%），而评分者身份仅贡献0.2%。在消融实验中，移除评分标准中的标准答案导致可靠性大幅下降（ICC从0.888降至0.628），证明评分标准（尤其是其中的标准答案）是使评分与模型智能解耦的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型用于考试评分时成本高昂，而传统假设认为小型模型难以胜任开放式答案评分。作者旨在验证一个设计原则：只要依据显式评分标准进行评分，低成本小型模型即可达到与昂贵模型相当的可靠性，从而大幅降低评分成本。

Method: 提出any-to-bench框架：(1)前沿模型在摄入阶段一次性读取源文档，提取每个问题及其评分标准；(2)低成本模型随后执行所有重复性评分工作。实验使用两个模型家族、三个推理努力等级、共6种低成本配置，对24道开放式考试题进行评分，每题3次评分共3,456个分数。通过方差分解分析答案身份与评分者身份对分数的影响，并通过两个消融实验（移除标准/答案）分解评分标准的贡献。

Result: 答案身份解释了95.6%的评分方差，而评分者身份仅解释0.2%。提高作者推理努力可使成绩变化高达满分的0.143，而提高评分者推理努力最多仅改变0.006。六个前沿模型作为对照组未表现出更高的面板可靠性。消融实验表明：保留标准答案时移除标准与等级无显著影响；而移除标准答案后可靠性崩塌（ICC从0.888降至0.628）、分数膨胀且评分者推理努力重新变得重要。未发现长度偏好或同族模型偏好。

Conclusion: 评分标准是分离评分与模型智能的关键因素，而在评分标准内部，标准答案承担了绝大部分工作。该发现支持低成本模型在rubric锚定下进行大规模可靠评分的可行性，为构建经济高效的自动化考试评估系统提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Small language models can grade open-ended examination answers as reliably as substantially more expensive models when they grade against an explicit rubric. We test this claim as the design principle behind any-to-bench: a frontier model reads source documents once, at ingestion, to extract each question and its rubric; lower-cost models then perform all repeated grading work. We evaluate six cost-efficient model configurations from two model families at three reasoning-effort levels. Each configuration answers 24 open-ended examination questions, and each also grades every answer sheet three times, yielding 3,456 per-question grades. Scores depend overwhelmingly on the answer being graded: answer identity explains 95.6% of score variance, whereas judge identity explains only 0.2%. Raising a writer's reasoning effort moves earned scores by as much as 0.143 of full marks, while raising a judge's reasoning effort moves assigned scores by at most 0.006. Six frontier-tier judges, added as a check, reproduce these scores and are no more reliable as a panel. Two ablations then decompose the rubric on the same questions and answers. Removing its criteria and levels while keeping the official answer changes nothing measurable. Removing the official answer as well collapses reliability (ICC 0.888 to 0.628), inflates scores, and makes judge reasoning effort matter again. The rubric is what decouples grading from judge intelligence, and within the rubric the official answer does nearly all the work. We find no evidence of length preference or same-family preference under rubric-anchored grading.

</details>


### [131] [Do Large Language Models Play Six Degrees of Separation? Measuring Topological Compression in Long-Context Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17950)
*Md. Faiyaz Abdullah Sayeedi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文绕开注意力权重，直接分析深层LLM隐状态流形的动态几何结构，证明其组织为Small-World网络。研究发现深层推理层存在拓扑相变，语义跳跃被压缩到"六度分隔"（≤6跳）内；并据此提出零样本幻觉检测方法，事实性生成与源上下文保持约3跳的结构完整性，而幻觉则导致严重拓扑坍缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于注意力的可解释性分析常因注意力汇聚（attention sinks）等路由伪影而失效，无法捕捉真实的语义邻近性，因此需要绕过注意力权重，直接从隐状态几何结构揭示LLM多跳推理的内部机制。

Method: 将长上下文的连续相似性矩阵稀疏化为无权图，在两个不同架构上追踪高度不连通语义锚点之间的连通性；分析隐状态流形的动态几何特征，验证其Small-World网络组织；并将该框架应用于RAGognize数据集上的零样本幻觉检测。

Result: 早期句法层完全碎片化，而深层推理层出现急剧拓扑相变，将大规模概念距离压缩为高度可导航的路径，且严格受"六度分隔"（≤6跳）限制。在幻觉检测中，事实性生成与源上下文保持约3跳的结构完整性，而幻觉诱导严重拓扑坍缩。

Conclusion: 该工作从数学上形式化了Transformer执行抽象推理的机制，提供了评估事实可靠性的全新几何签名，展示了几何框架在LLM可解释性与可靠性评估中的实用价值。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) demonstrate remarkable multi-hop reasoning capabilities over long contexts, yet the internal mechanisms enabling these distant cognitive leaps remain poorly understood. Traditional attention-based interpretability often fails to capture true semantic proximity due to routing artifacts like attention sinks. In this paper, we bypass attention weights to directly analyze the dynamic geometry of the hidden state manifold, proving that deep LLM latent spaces natively organize into Small-World networks. By sparsifying the continuous similarity matrices of long-context representations into unweighted graphs, we trace the connectivity between highly disjoint semantic anchors across two distinct architectures. Our findings reveal a sharp topological phase transition: while early syntactic layers remain entirely fractured, deep reasoning layers abruptly compress massive conceptual distances into highly navigable pathways strictly bounded by the "Six Degrees of Separation" limit (=< 6 semantic hops). Furthermore, we demonstrate the practical efficacy of this framework by applying it to zero-shot hallucination detection within Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) using the RAGognize dataset. We show that factually grounded generations maintain structural integrity with their source context (approximately 3 hops), whereas hallucinations induce severe topological collapse. Ultimately, this work mathematically formalizes how transformers execute abstract reasoning and provides a novel, strictly geometric signature for evaluating factual reliability.

</details>


### [132] [When Writing Style Drifts: Benchmarking Authorship Verification under Distribution Shifts in Genre, Time and the AI-Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17979)
*Lotta Kiefer,Brisca Balthes,Christoph Leiter,Yamen Ajjour,Elena Schmidt,Steffen Eger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AVShift是首个德语作者身份验证基准，包含超过15万对文本，跨越三种文体和21年时间跨度，统一评估跨文体、时间和AI时代三种分布偏移下的AV模型鲁棒性。研究发现微调LLM跨文体泛化最佳，时间偏移是影响AV的最强因素，而AI时代分布偏移未检测到显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AV基准通常孤立研究分布偏移因素且主要聚焦英语，无法全面反映模型在真实条件下的鲁棒性。作者身份稳定性假设在实践中受到文体、时间和AI辅助写作变化带来的分布偏移挑战，需要更全面的多偏移评估框架。

Method: 构建AVShift德语基准，包含超过150,000对文本，跨越三种文体（genre）和21年时间跨度，在统一框架下支持跨文体、时间和AI时代偏移的受控评估。对基于特征、基于嵌入和基于LLM的代表性方法进行基准测试，并进行特征稳定性分析。

Result: 1) 微调LLM在跨文体泛化上表现最佳，并显著受益于风格多样的训练数据；2) 时间漂移是影响AV的最强因素之一，随文档时间间隔增大性能显著下降；3) 未发现AVShift中存在可测量的AI时代分布偏移证据；4) 特征分析显示部分文体风格特征跨文体保持稳定，但其相对重要性因具体文体转换而异。

Conclusion: AVShift提供了首个德语多分布偏移AV评估框架，揭示了时间漂移对AV性能的关键影响以及LLM在跨文体泛化上的优势，并指出AI时代偏移在当前基准中不显著。相关基准和代码将开源供后续研究使用。

Abstract: Authorship verification (AV) assumes that an author's writing style remains sufficiently stable to distinguish it from that of other writers. In practice, however, this assumption is challenged by distribution shifts caused by changes in genre, time, and AI-assisted writing. Existing AV benchmarks typically study these factors in isolation and focus predominantly on English, limiting our understanding of model robustness under realistic conditions. We introduce AVShift, the first German benchmark for systematically evaluating AV under multiple distribution shifts. AVShift comprises over 150,000 text pairs spanning three genres and 21 years, enabling controlled evaluation of cross-genre, temporal, and AI-era shifts within a unified framework. We benchmark representative feature-based, embedding-based, and LLM-based approaches. Our experiments show that fine-tuned LLMs generalize best across genres and benefit substantially from stylistically diverse training data. We further demonstrate that temporal drift is one of the strongest factors affecting AV, with performance degrading significantly as the time gap between documents increases. In contrast, we find no evidence of a measurable AI-era distribution shift within AVShift. Finally, our feature analysis reveals stylistic features that remain stable across genres, while their relative importance varies depending on the specific genre transition. We release AVShift and our code for future research.

</details>


### [133] [Judge, Retrieve, or Abstain: Uncertainty-Guarded LLM Judging with Provable Risk Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17994)
*Sher Badshah,Ali Emami,Hassan Sajjad*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个风险受控的LLM评判框架，通过校准不确定性阈值来控制接受评判结果的错误发现率（FDR）在用户指定水平α以下，并在参数模式不自信时路由到检索增强模式收集网络证据重新评估，实现双阈值路由的有限样本保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为评判者已成为大规模评估模型输出的标准做法，尤其在主观开放任务中。然而在客观任务中，无参考答案的评判面临可靠性挑战：参数化知识模式可能产生幻觉或证据不足，而工具增强模式虽能提供额外证据但增加计算成本且需要可靠的调用机制。更重要的是，两种方法单独使用都无法对评判结果的风险提供形式化控制。

Method: 提出一个风险受控框架：1) 在保留集上使用有限样本Clopper-Pearson区间校准不确定性阈值，使接受评判结果中的错误发现率（FDR）以高概率保持在用户指定水平α以下；2) 当参数模式不够自信时，将实例路由到检索增强模式，评判者收集网络证据后用第二个校准阈值重新评估；3) 该有限样本保证无需额外假设即可延续到双阈值路由机制。

Result: 在开放域问答基准和不同规模的评判模型上，该框架在维持目标错误率的同时，覆盖率显著高于单一模式基线。

Conclusion: 该风险受控框架为无参考LLM评判提供了形式化的可靠性保证，通过参数模式与检索增强模式的结合，在控制错误发现率的同时最大化覆盖率，为客观任务的自动评估提供了更可靠的方案。

Abstract: Using LLMs as judges has become standard practice for evaluating model outputs at scale. This is particularly common for subjective, open-ended tasks such as assessing helpfulness or alignment, where no single reference answer exists. However, objective tasks introduce a distinct reliability challenge for reference-free LLM judging. In the absence of a reference answer, the judge evaluates factual correctness either through its parametric knowledge or through tool augmentation. Although the former enables efficient evaluation, the judge may hallucinate or lack sufficient evidence for its verdict. Conversely, tool augmentation can provide additional evidence but introduces extra computational cost and requires an appropriate mechanism to determine when and how that evidence should be used reliably. More importantly, neither approach alone provides formal control over the risk of accepted verdicts or guarantees their reliability at a specified level. We propose a risk-controlled framework that calibrates uncertainty thresholds on a held-out set so that the false discovery rate among accepted verdicts remains below a user-specified level~$α$ with high probability, using finite-sample Clopper--Pearson intervals. When the parametric mode is not sufficiently confident, the instance is routed to a retrieval-augmented mode, where the judge gathers web evidence and re-evaluates the instance under a second calibrated threshold. The finite-sample guarantee carries over to this two-threshold routing without additional assumptions. Across open-domain QA benchmarks and judges of varying scales, the framework maintains the target error rate while achieving substantially higher coverage than single-mode baselines.

</details>


### [134] [The IOL-AI Challenge: An Open Challenge towards Advancing Linguistic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18011)
*Eduardo Sánchez,Rita Berrada,Dan-Mircea Mirea,Sara Rajaee,Alexander Piperski,Ana Meta Dolinar,Boris Iomdin,Andrey Nikulin,Mariya Shmatova,Marzieh Fadaee,Julia Kreutzer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了IOL-AI挑战赛，一个基于2026年国际语言学奥林匹克竞赛（IOL）个人赛未公开题目的开放科学竞赛。731份提交来自46支队伍，在严格计算预算（单张T4、30分钟）下运行。结果显示Claude Opus 4.8获得金牌等效的评审分数，而资源受限系统仅处于参赛者底部5%。能力不受规模决定，自动评估与人工评审排序一致但压缩了分数差距。前沿模型对问题语言的先验知识并未显著帮助解决语言学推理任务，使语言学推理成为通用推理能力的强基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推理研究主要集中于数学和代码等领域，这些领域为模型提供了明确规则。然而语言学谜题要求解题者首先发现系统规则再进行推理，这与传统推理基准有本质区别。作者希望通过语言学奥林匹克题目来评估模型在规则发现与推理结合方面的能力，并弥补这一研究空白。

Method: 作者举办了IOL-AI挑战赛，使用IOL 2026个人赛的未公开题目作为基准，同时采用自动评估和官方IOL评审团（按照人类选手的评分标准）进行双重评估。竞赛设置了严格的计算约束（一张T4 GPU，30分钟），并对15个前沿和开放模型进行了无约束基准测试。他们还分析了不同规模模型的表现差异以及自动指标与人工评审之间的关系。

Result: ①Claude Opus 4.8获得等同于金牌的评审分数；②资源受限系统仅处于参赛者底部5%范围；③14B参数模型胜过两倍规模的模型，提升来自解码和输出处理而非模型容量；④自动指标与评审团排序一致，但压缩了分数尺度——低分系统被高估约13分，强系统被低估；⑤前沿模型对问题语言的先验知识并未显著帮助其解决语言学推理任务。

Conclusion: 语言学推理是评估LLM通用推理能力的强基准，与数学和代码等规则明确的领域形成互补。模型性能提升的关键在于解码策略和输出处理而非单纯的参数规模。自动评估虽然与人类评审排序一致，但存在分数压缩偏差，需要在未来的评估设计中加以校正。

Abstract: Reasoning in LLMs is overwhelmingly studied in domains that provide a model with rules: mathematics and code. Linguistic puzzles invert this: the solver must first discover the system before reasoning within it. We present the IOL-AI Challenge, an open-science competition run on the unseen problems of the International Linguistics Olympiad (IOL) 2026 Individual Contest, evaluated both automatically and, for the first time, by members of the official IOL Jury under the same rubrics applied to human contestants. The challenge drew 731 submissions from 46 teams under a strict compute budget (one T4, 30 mins). We additionally benchmark 15 unconstrained frontier and open models, with Claude Opus 4.8 earning a jury score equivalent to a gold medal, while both resource-constrained systems we submitted for jury grading scored in the range of the bottom 5% of contestants. Capability was not determined by scale: 14B submissions outperform models twice their size, and gains come from decoding and output-handling rather than model capacity. We also found that automatic metrics rank systems exactly as the jury does, but compress the scale, upscoring weak systems by ~13 points and understating strong ones. Our analysis shows that while frontier models might have prior knowledge about some of the problem languages, it does not significantly help them solve the linguistic reasoning tasks, leaving linguistic reasoning as a strong benchmarking proxy for generalizable reasoning skills.

</details>


### [135] [Chain-of-Experience for Continual LLM Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18027)
*Haoqin Tu,Yunhao Fang,Yizhong Wang,Cihang Xie,Shen Yan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Chain-of-Experience (CoE)框架，研究LLM在测试阶段通过迭代交互积累经验以持续改进的能力。实验涵盖数学、编程和知识领域，使用8个LLM（包括GPT-5、Gemini-2.5 Pro、Claude-4.5 Sonnet），发现迭代经验优于无反馈基线，自反馈带来显著增益（整体提升5.6%，API成本降低19%），组合多反馈通道可进一步提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类具有从经验中持续学习的能力，而传统LLM评估忽略模型通过推理时交互提升的可能性。现有的测试时策略大多基于零样本推理，缺乏让模型通过迭代反馈积累经验、形成持续改进循环的机制。

Method: 提出CoE（Chain-of-Experience）框架：模型在测试阶段通过迭代交互（与自身或环境反馈）累积经验痕迹，形成超越零样本推论的持续改进循环。实验纳入多种反馈机制（模型自反馈、正确性信号、编程测试通过率等），在数学、编码和知识领域使用8个LLM进行系统评估。

Result: 迭代经验一致优于无反馈基线：自反馈单独带来5.6%整体提升和19% API成本降低；组合互补反馈通道（模型+正确性信号）可获得额外增益；CoE在每token准确率上优于现有测试时策略；发现LLM基础能力与改进能力正相关；模型在弱/虚假反馈下保持稳健；不同反馈贡献不同改进方面，且多数增益在迭代早期出现。

Conclusion: CoE验证了LLM具备从推理时迭代经验中持续学习的能力，为测试时计算策略提供了新范式，表明通过反馈驱动的经验积累可以高效提升模型性能，同时兼具成本效益和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Humans continuously learn from experience, whereas conventional large language model (LLM) evaluations ignore the models' ability to improve through inference-time interaction. In this paper, we study how LLMs learn from iterative experience at test time, a setting we refer to as Chain-of-Experience (CoE), where models accumulate experiential traces through iterative interactions with self or environmental feedback to form a continual improvement loop beyond zero-shot inference. We instantiate CoE with diverse feedback mechanisms, including model self-feedback and environmental signals such as correctness or public coding test pass rates, and evaluate across math, coding, and knowledge domains using 8 LLMs, including GPT-5, Gemini-2.5 Pro, Claude-4.5 Sonnet. Our study shows that leveraging iterative experience consistently outperforms feedback-free baselines, achieving substantial gains with self feedback alone, alongside a 5.6% overall improvement and 19% lower API cost across tasks and models. We further show that combining complementary feedback channels (e.g., model and correctness signals) yields additional gains, and that CoE delivers higher accuracy per token than existing test-time strategies. We observe a positive correlation between LLM base ability and improvement capacity, and show that models remain robust under weak or spurious feedback, with different feedback contributing to distinct improvement aspects and most gains emerging early in the iterations.

</details>


### [136] [Language Has Two Parameters: Narrative-Induced Semantic Plasticity and Phase-Sensitive Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18041)
*Hollis Robbins*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出语言中存在第二个参数"相位"(phase)，用于描述语义如何组合，区别于传统基于词频和embedding的幅度(amplitude)参数。相位关系只存在于意义之间，决定共激活意义如何组合，即使意义保持完全在场也能逆转其贡献。由于群体平均会删除历史索引的相位，标准transformer在冷冻推理中没有显式相位表征，而可解释性研究中以单语义性衡量的进展实际上与相位相悖。论文提出六个预测检验该理论，并主张弱版本：解释需要第二个关系参数（有符号的、持久的、索引到个体和二元组的），而强版本（量子演算约束这些现象）尚未得到充分推导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型和可解释性研究认为通过词频统计、embedding和注意力权重就能捕捉语言的全部意义关联，但这些方法将所有作者在语料库中求和，失去了个体历史信息。作者认为这遗漏了一个关键维度——相位，即意义间如何组合的关系参数。这个遗漏导致标准的transformer架构无法处理反讽、引语、暗示等需要意义共存的语言现象，而单语义性(monosemanticity)解释目标恰恰与这种共存条件相矛盾。

Method: 论文从理论上提出"相位"(phase)作为聚合计数(amplitude)之外的第二语言参数。相位存在于意义之间，决定共激活意义如何组合，可通过语言形式在信号中设置，通过遭遇(encounter)安装相位关系，由历史分布在个体间。论文论证了群体平均会删除历史索引的相位，因此代理去索引化的语料库只能识别群体边际状态。论文提出六项可测试预测：(1)被抑制的意义是否保持活跃；(2)遭遇顺序是否改变短语功能；(3)信号标记是否改变共享短语的理解方式；(4)模型是否会被历史改变而非仅被告知。最后建议使用量子概率作为该参数的记号（不声称大脑存在量子过程），并指出弱版本（需要第二个关系参数）和强版本（量子演算约束这些现象）的区别。

Result: 论文的"结果"主要是理论性主张和预测：(1)标准transformer在冷冻推理中没有显式相位表征；(2)群体平均（代理去索引化）确定不了任何个体或双人组状态；(3)单语义性解释进展实际上在优化对抗相位所需的条件；(4)提出六项预测来检验被抑制意义是否保持活跃、遭遇顺序是否改变功能、信号标记是否改变理解、模型是否被历史改变；(5)弱版本主张被辩护：解释需要有符号的、持久的、索引到个体和二元组的第二个关系参数；(6)强版本搁置，因为遭遇顺序约束尚未推导出来。

Conclusion: 语言解释需要第二个关系参数——相位，它是有符号的、持久的、索引到个体和二元组的。这一主张的弱版本可以成立：仅靠聚合计数的语义模型不足以解释暗示、反讽和引语等现象。该理论要求的架构是带有代理索引、承载相位的语义状态的语言模型。强版本（量子演算约束这些现象）尚未推导出来，是未来工作方向。

Abstract: Language has two parameters. Count how often words occur together and you estimate amplitude, the strength of association. Word embeddings and attention weights refine that count, which sums every writer in the corpus together. This paper claims a second parameter, phase, which signed weights learned from a corpus do not supply. Phase exists only between meanings: it determines how coactivated meanings combine, and it can reverse what a meaning contributes while that meaning stays fully present. A speaker can set phase in the signal through linguistic form; encounters install phase relations and history distributes them. Population averaging deletes history-indexed phase: agent-deindexed corpora identify the population marginal state and determine no individual or dyadic state, at any scale. The standard transformer has no explicit representation for phase in frozen inference, and the interpretability program measuring progress by monosemanticity is optimizing against it: the coexistence it treats as a defect is the condition of allusion, irony, and quotation. Six predictions test whether a suppressed meaning stays active, whether encounter order changes what a phrase does, whether marking the signal changes how a shared phrase is taken, and whether a model given a history is changed by it or only informed about it. The claim defended is the weak version: interpretation requires a second relational parameter, signed, persistent, and indexed to individuals and dyads. Quantum probability is one notation for the parameter; nothing in the formalism claims quantum processes in the brain. The strong version, that the quantum calculus constrains these phenomena as signed classical models do not, rests on an encounter-order constraint not yet derived. The architecture the theory calls for is a language model with agent-indexed, phase-bearing semantic states.

</details>


### [137] [TokEval: A Tokenizer Evaluation Suite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18062)
*Clara Meister*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TokEval框架，一套超越传统指标（如压缩率）的tokenizer评估指标体系，通过受控预训练实验验证这些指标与下游模型性能的关联，发现信息论指标预测语言建模能力（Spearman rho高达0.80），而结构敏感指标（如数字和换行处理）与任务准确率相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型tokenizer的选择通常缺乏充分评估，其设计直接-impact模型能力但理解有限。现有指标（如fertility和压缩率）不足以捕捉tokenizer对语言、数学、代码等不同领域能力的影响，导致tokenizer选择缺乏原则性指导。

Method: 提出TokEval框架，包含超越传统度量的评估指标，如UTF-8字符边界完整性、数字位值边界对齐等语言学与结构有意义属性。通过控制变量法进行预训练实验，仅改变tokenizer的训练数据混合、预分词策略和训练算法，在bits-per-byte和多个基准（语言理解、数学推理、代码生成）上评估模型。

Result: 实验表明不同内在属性对不同模型能力有不同影响：信息理论指标能预测语言建模能力（Spearman rho高达0.80）；结构敏感指标（如数字和换行处理度量）与任务准确率相关。

Conclusion: TokEval有望实现更原则化的tokenizer评估，在内在测量与预训练扫描结果一致的情况下，用内在测量替代昂贵的预训练sweeps。

Abstract: Language model tokenizers are typically selected with minimal evaluation, despite the fact that their design choices directly impact model capabilities. This can be partly attributed to a limited understanding of which tokenizer properties affect which aspects of downstream performance. We introduce TokEval, a framework of tokenizer evaluation metrics that goes beyond standard measures like fertility and compression rate to capture linguistically and structurally meaningful properties, e.g., UTF-8 character boundary integrity and digit place-value boundary alignment for mathematics. To validate whether these metrics are predictive of downstream model performance, we conduct controlled language model pretraining experiments, varying solely the tokenizers' training data mixture, pretokenization strategy, and training algorithm. We evaluate the resulting models on bits-per-byte (a tokenizer-agnostic version of perplexity) and several benchmarks, spanning linguistic understanding, mathematical reasoning, and code generation. Our experiments suggest that different intrinsic properties have different impacts on model abilities: information-theoretic metrics predict language modeling abilities (Spearman rho up to 0.80), while structure-sensitive metrics, such as those measuring digit and line-break handling, correlate with task accuracy. We hope TokEval enables more principled tokenizer evaluation, replacing pretraining sweeps with intrinsic measurement wherever the two agree.

</details>


### [138] [Multi-Agent AI System for Radiology Report Structuring and Quality Assurance with Independent Radiologist Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18072)
*Iryna Hartsock,Cesar Lam,Christopher Otteni,Aliya Qayyum,Robert Gatenby,Cyrillo Araujo,Ghulam Rasool*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究开发并评估了一个本地部署的多智能体AI系统，用于放射学报告的结构化和质量保证。该系统成功将638份CT报告的22,270个句子结构化到预定义的解剖学格式中，并标记了90份（14.1%）存在质量问题的报告。两位放射科医生评估显示69%的报告被正确重构，无临床重要信息遗漏或捏造内容，84%的报告QA性能被评为"优秀"或"良好"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放射学报告在临床实践中至关重要，但传统报告往往缺乏标准化结构，且质量保证依赖人工审查，耗时费力。本研究旨在通过本地部署的多智能体AI系统，在同一工作流程中整合报告结构化和质量保证，以提高放射实践的标准化水平和质量。

Method: 研究采用回顾性设计，纳入2023-2024年由15位认证放射科医生口述的638份胸腹盆CT检查报告。开发了一个多智能体AI流水线，使用正则规则和本地大语言模型在句子级别将报告结构化为标准解剖学部分，并检测发现与印象部分之间或部分内部的不匹配、性别-解剖学冲突，以及未记录的危急发现沟通。由两位认证放射科医生独立评估45份报告的子集。

Result: 系统成功将所有报告的22,270个句子结构化到预定义的解剖学格式，同时保留原始报告内容。共标记90份（14.1%）报告存在质量问题，最常见的是部分不匹配（80份，12.5%）。放射科医生评估中，两位审阅者一致认为31份（69%）正确重构，2份（4%）错误重构，12份（27%）意见不一。双方一致认为没有遗漏临床重要信息或引入捏造内容。84%的评估报告QA性能被评为"优秀"或"良好"。

Conclusion: 本地部署的多智能体AI系统成功地将放射学报告结构化和质量保证整合到单一工作流程中。系统在放射科医生评估中表现出良好性能，可能支持放射实践中报告标准化和质量保证的推广。

Abstract: Purpose: To develop and evaluate a locally deployed multi-agent AI system for radiology report structuring and quality assurance. Materials and Methods: This retrospective study included 638 radiology reports from CT examinations of the chest, abdomen, and pelvis dictated by 15 board-certified radiologists in 2023 and 2024. A multi-agent AI pipeline was developed to perform report structuring and quality assurance (QA). The system structured the report into standardized anatomical sections at the sentence level using regex rules and local large language models. It also detected mismatches between the Findings and Impression sections, or within sections; gender-anatomy conflicts; and undocumented communication of critical findings. Two board-certified radiologists independently evaluated a 45-report subset. Results: The multi-agent system structured the Findings sections of all reports (22,270 sentences) into a predefined anatomical format while retaining the original report content. The system flagged 90 (14.1%) reports, most commonly for section mismatches (80 reports, 12.5%). In the radiologist evaluation, both reviewers agreed that 31 (69%) were correctly restructured, 2 reports (4%) were incorrectly restructured, and disagreed on the remaining 12 reports (27%). Both reviewers agreed that no clinically important information was omitted and no fabricated content was introduced. Overall QA performance was rated as "excellent" or "good" in 84% of the evaluated reports, with the remaining reports rated as "fair". Conclusion: A locally deployed multi-agent AI system combined radiology report structuring and quality assurance within a single workflow. The system demonstrated favorable performance in radiologist evaluation. Such systems may support standardization of reporting and quality assurance in radiology practice.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [139] [ORCA: Observability-Grounded Program Repair for Microservice Incidents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17018)
*Yuanchen Gao,Yifang Tian,Yiran Li,Charles Zhang,Hans-Arno Jacobsen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ORCA是一个面向微服务事故的、基于可观测性（telemetry）的程序自动修复（APR）流水线，它将失败与参考遥测数据的差异提炼为故障签名，用于定位候选代码和部署配置位置，并通过修复图代理和探索代理生成补丁，最后由遥测接地验证器评估补丁。在575个案例基准上，ORCA在成本效益上优于所有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微服务故障通常从运维遥测数据中诊断，但现有的自动程序修复系统通常从issue报告、局部代码上下文或失败测试出发。这种不匹配导致基于遥测的诊断与补丁生成之间存在鸿沟，需要一种将运维遥测转化为修复上下文的方案。

Method: ORCA包含四个核心组件：(1) 将配对失败与参考遥测的差异提炼为故障签名；(2) 利用故障签名定位候选代码和部署配置位置；(3) 修复图代理（Repair graph agents）与探索代理（Exploration agent）从这些位置生成统一差异补丁候选；(4) 遥测接地补丁验证器（Telemetry-Grounded Patch Verifier），分别评估补丁有效性、语法与语义正确性、测试预言完整性和遥测重放。

Result: 在575例基准测试中，ORCA在成本效益（cost-effectiveness）方面优于所有评估的基线方法。结果表明运维遥测可以成功地从诊断证据转化为可操作的修复上下文。

Conclusion: 配对遥测数据支持面向修复的定位，修复图代理可将局部代码与配置证据转化为LLM受限的补丁生成上下文，而遥测接地验证能够揭示仅依靠issue或测试评估会遗漏的修复结果。这表明遥测驱动的APR是可行且有效的。

Abstract: Microservice failures are often diagnosed from operational telemetry. However, automated program repair systems usually start from issue reports, localized code context, or failing tests. This mismatch leaves a gap between telemetry-based diagnosis and patch generation. We present ORCA, an observability-grounded APR pipeline for microservice incidents. ORCA first distills the differences in paired failure and reference telemetry into a fault signature, then uses the signature to identify candidate code and deployment-configuration locations. Repair graph agents and an Exploration agent generate unified-diff patch candidates from these locations. ORCA evaluates generated patches with a Telemetry-Grounded Patch Verifier that separates patch validity, syntactic and semantic correctness, test-oracle integrity, and telemetry replay. On a 575-case benchmark, ORCA outperforms all evaluated baselines in terms of cost-effectiveness. Results show that operational telemetry can be transformed from diagnostic evidence into actionable repair context: paired telemetry supports repair-oriented localization, while repair graph agents convert localized code and configuration evidence into constrained patch-generation context for the LLM. Telemetry-grounded verification then exposes repair outcomes that issue- or test-only evaluation would miss.

</details>


### [140] [LadderTeam: Dual-Agent Laddering Elicitation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17029)
*Manjushree Aithal,Alexander Kotz,James Mitchell*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出LadderTeam框架，利用双智能体大语言模型架构自动化UX线框图访谈中的阶梯访谈（laddering interview）技术，通过主动访谈者和后台评判者两个智能体协同工作，在216场对照模拟实验中达到99.1%的链收敛率和81.0%的接地真值可行动回复匹配率，且零漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从终端用户收集详细且可操作的软件需求是迭代开发的关键阶段。虽然阶梯访谈技术能有效保证反馈的细致性和可操作性，但传统手工执行方式存在时间和财务成本高、扩展性受限的问题；同时访谈者需要在追问深度与应对受访者行为和文化约束之间取得平衡。这些局限性促使研究者探索自动化解决方案。

Method: 提出LadderTeam框架，采用双智能体LLM架构：(1) 主动访谈者智能体执行三种追问策略（ACV、5-Whys、JTBD）从可用性反馈评论中 eliciting 可操作的软件需求；(2) 并行的后台评判者智能体评估问答对并触发实时护栏以防止话题漂移。为严谨评估，引入受控模拟方法，使用脚本化的接地真值转录本隔离访谈质量作为唯一实验变量。

Result: 在216场访谈中，LadderTeam实现了99.1%的链收敛率、81.0%的接地真值可行动回复匹配率（犹豫型人格86.1%，简洁型人格75.9%），且所有运行零漂移。

Conclusion: LadderTeam通过双智能体架构有效自动化了阶梯访谈过程，克服了传统方法的扩展性瓶颈，同时通过实时护栏保证了访谈方向不偏离。该框架开放、可复现，所有评估代码、转录本、输入和演示平台将开源。

Abstract: Eliciting detailed and actionable software requirements from end-users is a critical phase in the iterative development of a software product or application. To ensure the feedback collected is detailed and actionable, software teams can leverage the laddering interview technique. While effective for ensuring granular and actionable items from the software feedback, these interviews are subject to several limitations. They are traditionally a manual process associated with a time and financial burden, limiting scalability; interviewers must balance probing for depth while managing interviewee behavioral and cultural constraints. To address these limitations, we present \textbf{LadderTeam}, an open, reproducible framework that automates UX wireframe interviews using a dual-agent Large Language Model (LLM) architecture. An active interviewer agent executes one of three probing strategies (ACV, 5-Whys, and JTBD) to elicit actionable software requirements from usability feedback comments, while a concurrent background Judge agent evaluates probe-response pairs and triggers real-time guardrails to prevent topic drift. To rigorously evaluate LLM laddering without participant variance confounds, we introduce a controlled simulation methodology utilizing scripted ground-truth transcripts to isolate probe quality as the sole experimental variable. Across 216 interviews, \textbf{LadderTeam} achieved 99.1\% chain convergence and an 81.0\% ground-truth actionable response match (86.1\% reluctant personality, 75.9\% terse personality) with zero drift across all runs. All evaluation code, all transcripts, inputs, and a live demonstration platform will be open-sourced upon acceptance.

</details>


### [141] [A Multi-Surface Consistency Audit of Software Citation Metadata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17159)
*Pengyin Shan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对117个开源研究软件项目进行了审计，比较每个项目多达7个机器可读元数据表面（如CITATION.cff、DOI注册记录、包注册表、README等）之间的一致性。结果显示83.9%的项目在核心字段上存在冲突，约半数冲突源于"描述论文而非软件本身"这一单一机制，且作者列表和标题分歧最大，注册表表面最不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究软件项目的元数据分散在多个平台（引用文件、归档库、DOI注册记录、包注册表、README等），不同消费方（引文指南、索引服务、自动化代理）可能读取不同子集，导致各表面之间的不一致会悄无声息地割裂学术归属和溯源信息。此前从未直接量化过"项目自身元数据表面之间的一致性程度"这一基本问题。

Method: 对117个项目（87个高性能计算/量子计算项目 + 30个来自JOSS和pyOpenSci的注册基线项目）进行了审计，每个项目最多检查7个机器可读表面。使用四级判定标准跨6个元数据字段进行评估，采用模糊匹配阈值，并在338行分层样本上进行了98.5%的人工验证精度校验。还进行了半人工裁决分析冲突根因，并发布了完整的审计流水线、语料库和验证日志。

Result: 62个暴露至少两个可比表面的项目中，52个（83.9%）存在至少一个核心字段冲突，且该结果对模糊匹配阈值不敏感。手工裁决的跨表面冲突中，一半源于单一机制：表面描述的是软件的论文而非软件本身。32个含倾向引用的项目中，28个（87.5%）的引用路由指向与软件自身元数据不一致的记录。作者列表和标题分歧最大，注册表表面最不一致。

Conclusion: 研究软件项目自身的元数据表面之间存在系统性且普遍的不一致性，这会严重破坏软件的信誉和归属追踪。作者呼吁关注"表面分歧"问题，并通过发布完整审计工具链推动该问题的可复现评估与改进。

Abstract: Research software projects describe themselves in many places at once: citation files in the repository, archive deposits, DOI registry records, package registries, and README text. We treat the software as the underlying object and these machine-readable self-descriptions as its surfaces: the points where people and automated systems read what the project declares about the software. Citation guidance, indexing services, and automated agents may read a different subset of these surfaces, so disagreement between them can silently fragment credit and provenance. This paper asks a simple question that has not been measured directly: when a project's own metadata surfaces are compared with each other, how often do they agree? We audited 117 open-source research software projects, comprising an 87-project high-performance computing and quantum computing corpus and a 30-project registered baseline drawn from the JOSS and pyOpenSci accepted-package lists, across up to seven machine-readable surfaces per project. Using a four-level verdict rubric across six metadata fields, with 98.5\% hand-verified verdict precision on a 338-row stratified sample, we found that 52 of the 62 projects exposing at least two comparable surfaces (83.9\%) contain at least one core-field conflict, a result that is insensitive to the fuzzy-matching threshold. Half of hand-adjudicated cross-surface conflicts trace to a single mechanism: surfaces describing the software's paper rather than the software itself. Among projects whose CITATION.cff includes a preferred citation, 28 of 32 route citations to a record that disagrees with the software's own metadata. The author lists and titles disagree the most, and the registry surfaces are the least aligned. We release the audit pipeline as an importable library, the corpus, the registered sampling protocol, all raw snapshots, and the complete verification log.

</details>


### [142] [Grounding AI Agents in Contracts: An Empirical Evaluation of Spec-Driven Test Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17177)
*Michele Tufano,James McClure,José Cambronero,Runxiang Cheng,Sherry Y. Shi,Renyao Wei,Dorothy Chen,Franjo Ivančić,Livio Dalloro,Pat Rondon*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"规范驱动测试生成"（Spec-Driven Test Generation）方法，让LLM智能体先生成代码的前置条件、后置条件和未定义行为等半形式化规范，以此作为认知脚手架来引导后续测试生成。在Google生产环境bug上的评估显示，该方法在bug检测率上提升9.8个百分点（p=0.0352），分支覆盖率提升2.5个百分点（p=0.0034），且生成的测试套件在77.8%和56.7%的情况下分别优于基线和人类编写的测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在直接生成测试时，往往无法充分推理代码及其底层契约，导致遗漏边界情况和行为边界，从而影响测试质量。

Method: 让智能体先生成并显式记录代码的前置条件、后置条件和未定义行为，形成半形式化规范作为中间步骤（认知脚手架），再基于该规范指导测试生成。

Result: 在Google生产环境bug上的评估中，规范驱动智能体相比传统测试生成基线，bug检测率提升9.8个百分点（p=0.0352），分支覆盖率提升2.5个百分点（p=0.0034）。LLM-as-a-Judge评估显示，其生成的测试套件在77.8%和56.7%的情况下分别优于基线和人类编写的测试，并在最佳实践遵循、可读性和边界用例覆盖上均有改进。

Conclusion: 通过先显式推理和记录代码契约，可以有效提升LLM智能体的测试生成质量，该方法在bug检测、分支覆盖率及测试套件整体质量上均显著优于传统基线，甚至超越部分人类编写的测试。

Abstract: LLM-based agents are increasingly used for coding tasks, where they have outperformed many classical approaches and scaled to repository-level tasks, such as test generation. However, when directly prompted to generate tests, these agents can fail to reason about the code and its underlying contracts, thereby missing edge cases and behavioral boundaries that affect test quality. To address this limitation, we propose Spec-Driven Test Generation, where we instruct an agent to first reason about -- and explicitly document -- code pre-conditions, post-conditions, and undefined behaviors. This intermediate semi-formal specification acts as a cognitive scaffold to guide subsequent test generation. Our evaluation on production bugs from Google shows that the spec-driven agent can deliver a 9.8 percentage points ($p = 0.0352$) improvement in bug detection rate and a 2.5 percentage point ($p = 0.0034$) improvement in branch coverage, compared to a traditional test generation agent baseline. Using LLM-as-a-Judge, we further show that test suites generated by the spec-driven agent are superior to the baseline and human-authored tests in 77.8% and 56.7% of the cases, respectively, and demonstrated improvements on following best practices, readability, and edge-case coverage.

</details>


### [143] [Graphectory Viewer: A Tool for Process-Centric Analysis of Agentic Software Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17195)
*Charlie Jyu,Shuyang Liu,Reyhaneh Jabbarvand*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Graphectory Viewer，一个基于Web的交互式工具，用于对软件智能体轨迹进行以过程为中心的分析。该工具基于先前工作中提出的Graphectory表示，将异构原始轨迹转换为感知阶段的图结构，连接底层执行细节与高层行为结构，支持多框架轨迹、交互式图构建、节点级检查、搜索过滤和Sankey式阶段转换摘要，并作为开源工具发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件智能体（如LLM驱动的代理）在执行复杂任务时产生的轨迹数据庞大且异构，传统分析仅关注最终任务结果，缺乏对执行过程的深入理解。需要一种能够将原始轨迹转化为结构化、可交互分析的表示和工具，以帮助研究者和实践者检查个体执行、识别行为模式、比较成功与失败案例，并分析大规模轨迹语料库。

Method: Graphectory Viewer建立在之前的Graphectory图表示之上，将异构原始轨迹转换为"阶段感知"（phase-aware）图结构。核心功能包括：(1) 支持来自多个智能体框架的轨迹输入；(2) 交互式图构建；(3) 节点级检查（思想、行动、观察）；(4) 对大规模轨迹集合的搜索与过滤；(5) Sankey式问题解决阶段转换摘要。工具以Web形式提供，便于可视化分析。

Result: 该工具能够实现个体执行过程的深入检查，识别重复出现的行为模式，比较成功与失败的运行轨迹，并对超出最终任务结果的大规模轨迹语料进行分析。作为开源工件发布，附带文档、预计算图和大型轨迹语料库，支持可复现性和后续研究。

Conclusion: Graphectory Viewer提供了一种从过程视角分析软件智能体轨迹的实用工具，弥补了传统仅以结果为导向的分析方法的不足。通过开源发布和相关资源，该工具为智能体行为研究和实践分析提供了可扩展、可复现的平台。

Abstract: We present Graphectory Viewer, a web-based tool for interactive, process-centric analysis of software-agent trajectories. Building on the Graphectory representation introduced in our previous work, Graphectory Viewer transforms heterogeneous raw trajectories into phase-aware graphs that connect low-level execution details with higher-level behavioral structures. The tool supports trajectories from multiple agent frameworks and provides interactive graph construction; node-level inspection of thoughts, actions, and observations; search and filtering over large trajectory collections; and Sankey-style summaries of problem-solving phase transitions. These capabilities enable researchers and practitioners to inspect individual executions, identify recurring behavioral patterns, compare successful and failed runs, and analyze large trajectory corpora beyond final task outcomes. To support reproducibility and further research, we release Graphectory Viewer as an open-source artifact together with documentation, precomputed graphs, and the large-scale trajectory corpus.

</details>


### [144] [Oracles That Cannot Fail: Anchoring and the Expectation That Moves With the Fault](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17214)
*Arquimedes Canedo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"oracle anchoring"（预言机锚定）概念：当测试预言机的期望值来自被测系统自身时，故障若同时移动测量值和期望值，比较会被完全抵消，导致故障无法被任何输入暴露。作者在部署的空中交通管制模拟器上测量了该现象，并提出检测和修复方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试预言机（oracle）从被测系统中获得期望值时，若故障使测量与期望一起移动，比较被完全抵消，缺陷无法被任何输入揭示。该问题属于预言机本身而非输入空间。期望值形式在测试气味文献中被命名但从未被测量，作者希望填补这一空白并量化其影响。

Method: 作者将期望值分为"specification-anchored"（由被测代码外部固定值组成）和"state-anchored"（直接或传递地来自被测代码）。命名三种额外的值到达判定结果的通道，将谓词限制为从变异目标流入的值，并在4个模块、366个变异体上测量。随后进行三次干预实验：重新锚定一个预言机、状态锚定一个健康的去抖预言机、以及用参考模型对比。

Result: 在12个无模型属性套件、4个模块、366个变异体上，比手写测试多检测3个变异体，且每个测试效率高6-33倍。三次干预中：重新锚定恢复8/46；状态锚定健康预言机损失4/19；参考模型与规格锚定杀死完全相同的变异体。消融的6个实例中，两个决定比较规模的实例承载了12个恢复变异体中的11个。发布的测试气味规则会撤销修复。

Conclusion: 预言机锚定是真实存在的、可测量的测试预言机缺陷。发表的测试气味规则与实际修复方向相反；写入分析认为缺失的预言机后暴露了两个部署未发现的缺陷。作者承认所有测量来自单一系统、单一作者，存在普遍性局限。

Abstract: A test oracle that obtains its expected value from the system it is judging cannot fail. If a fault moves measurement and expectation together the comparison cancels exactly, and no generated input will reveal it. The defect is in the oracle and not in the input space. We call this oracle anchoring. An expectation is specification-anchored when composed from values fixed outside the code under mutation, and state-anchored when it flows, directly or transitively, from that code. The expected-value form is named in the test-smell literature but not measured in any study we retrieved. We name three further channels by which such a value reaches a verdict, restrict the predicate to values flowing from the mutate target, and measure it. The subject is a deployed air traffic control simulator with 12 model-free property suites. Across 4 modules and 366 mutants these add 3 mutants of detection over the hand-written tests, while remaining 6 to 33 times as efficient per test. We then intervene three times, predicting each outcome first. Re-anchoring one holding oracle on published procedure, changing no production code, recovers 8 of 46; state-anchoring a healthy debounce oracle costs 4 of 19; and a reference model on that population kills exactly what specification anchoring kills, placing the risk in anchoring and not in model-freedom. Of 6 instances ablated, the two sizing their comparison carry 11 of the 12 recovered mutants. The published smell rule would revert our repair. Writing the oracle this analysis said was missing then exposed two defects deployment had not surfaced. All measurements come from one system by one author.

</details>


### [145] [SNIPTEST: Fuzzing Multi-Level Code Slices for Validating Vulnerabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17396)
*Aniruddhan Murali,Nobble Saji Mathews,Mahmoud Alfadel,Meng Xu,Meiyappan Nagappan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SNIPTEST是一个基于执行的警告分类框架，通过生成并模糊测试围绕静态分析警告的编译代码切片，使用逐层扩展的切片策略来验证潜在漏洞，在97个真实漏洞和97个误报的基准测试中分别产生了54.6%和55.6%的证据，并成功识别了CVE-2025-11964。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态分析工具会产生大量需要人工检查的警告，而将定向模糊测试应用于整个项目来验证每个警告在计算上不可行，通常需要数天执行时间且只能获得增量式的代码覆盖率改进。

Method: SNIPTEST采用执行基础的警告分类框架，生成并模糊测试围绕静态分析警告的编译代码切片。它使用逐层切片策略，围绕目标位置逐渐扩展上下文，以递增的精度验证潜在漏洞，而非在完整程序中证明可利用性。

Result: 在97个真实漏洞和97个误报的基准测试中，SNIPTEST对53个（54.6%）已确认漏洞产生可能的真阳性证据，其中40.2%的案例沿观察到的执行路径利用了漏洞并匹配前三个栈帧；对54个（55.6%）已确认误报产生可能的假阳性证据，但误分类了28个案例（28.8%）。此外成功识别了CVE-2025-11964。

Conclusion: SNIPTEST通过切片和逐步扩展上下文的策略，为静态分析警告提供了可行的执行基础分类方法，在平衡计算成本与验证精度的同时展示了实际漏洞识别能力，但仍有部分案例无法覆盖或存在误分类。

Abstract: Modern software systems are increasingly complex, and static analysis tools are commonly used to identify potentially vulnerable code by issuing warnings. However, these warnings often require manual inspection to confirm whether the reported issues are real, making the process time-consuming and error-prone. Directed fuzzing has emerged as a powerful automated technique to validate the warnings. However, applying it to the entire project in response to each warning is computationally infeasible, often requiring days of execution to achieve only incremental improvements in code coverage.
  We present SNIPTEST, an execution-based warning triage framework that generates and fuzzes compiled code slices centered around static-analysis warnings. Rather than proving exploitability in the full program, SNIPTEST provides evidence about how a warning behaves under progressively expanded sliced execution contexts. It employs a layer-by-layer slicing strategy, incrementally expanding context around the target location to validate potential vulnerabilities with increasing precision. We evaluate SNIPTEST on a benchmark of 97 true vulnerabilities and 97 false alarms across three real-world projects. SNIPTEST produces Possible True Positive evidence for 53 of 97 confirmed vulnerabilities (54.6%) by triggering the corresponding bug oracle consistently across all three analyzed slice levels, while the remaining cases are unreachable. Particularly, in 40.2% of these cases, it exploits the vulnerability along the observed execution path, matching the top three stack frames. On the 97 confirmed false alarms, SNIPTEST produces Possible False Positive evidence for 54 cases (55.6%) by reaching the warning without triggering the bug oracle, but misclassifies 28 cases (28.8%),and the remaining cases are unreached. Finally, we demonstrate the practical relevance of SNIPTEST by identifying CVE-2025-11964.

</details>


### [146] [COMMITGUARD: Differential Slice Fuzzing for Commit-Induced Bug Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17401)
*Aniruddhan Murali,Noble Saji Mathews,Mahmoud Alfadel,Meiyappan Nagappan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: COMMITGUARD 提出了一种基于提交感知的差分切片模糊测试方法，通过对比提交前后的函数切片来验证代码变更，将大规模模糊测试产生的报告精确筛选为提交引入的bug。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代软件频繁提交代码变更，但代码审查和测试对内存安全问题的保障有限。对每个提交进行全程序模糊测试代价高昂且难以覆盖变更代码，需要一个更高效的提交级验证方法。

Method: COMMITGUARD 识别每个提交修改的函数，从提交前后版本分别提取可编译的代码切片，独立对配对的切片进行模糊测试，然后对比两个版本的 sanitizer 报告，仅将提交后版本新出现的 bug 报告为候选的提交引入 bug。

Result: 在 openSSL、libpcap 和 leptonica 的 300 个提交上评估，初始产生 518 个 sanitizer 报告，最终筛选出 7 个候选提交引入 bug，人工验证确认 5 个为真实 bug，平均分析一个提交需 32.4 分钟，平均覆盖修改函数 75.36% 的代码。

Conclusion: COMMITGUARD 通过提交感知的差分切片模糊测试，能够高效地将大规模模糊测试输出缩小到少量需要人工审核的提交引入 bug 报告，验证了该方法在发现真实提交引入的内存安全缺陷方面的有效性。

Abstract: Modern software systems evolve through frequent commits that implement bug fixes, features, and security patches. Although code review and testing are widely used to check these changes, they often provide limited assurance for memory-safety issues. Code reviewers may miss subtle boundary, lifetime, or initialization errors, while existing tests may not exercise the specific paths affected by a commit. Fuzzing is effective at exposing such bugs, but applying it to every commit remains impractical because whole-program fuzzing is expensive, requires suitable harnesses, and may still fail to reach the code changed by a commit.
  In this paper, we introduce COMMITGUARD, a commit-aware differential slice-based fuzzing approach for verifying code changes. The key insight behind COMMITGUARD is that the pre-commit version of a modified function can serve as a behavioral baseline for interpreting bugs found after the commit. For each target commit, COMMITGUARD identifies modified functions, extracts compilable code slices from both the pre-commit and post-commit versions, and fuzzes the paired slices independently. It then compares sanitizer reports across the two versions and reports bugs that emerge only in the post-commit version as candidate commit-induced bugs. We evaluate COMMITGUARD on 300 commits from openSSL, libpcap and leptonica. Slice fuzzing initially produces 518 sanitizer reports across these commits. By comparing pre-commit and post-commit slices, COMMITGUARD narrows this large output to 7 candidate commit-induced bug reports that require manual triage. Manual validation confirms 5 of these reports as real bugs that were fixed by developers of the examined projects after we reported them, while only 2 reports were classified as false positives. COMMITGUARD analyzes a commit in 32.4 minutes on average and achieves 75.36% average coverage of modified functions.

</details>


### [147] [Beyond FLOPs: Energy-Aware Knowledge Distillation for Sustainable LLMs on Code-Related Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17515)
*Enrique Barba Roque,Luís Cruz,Annibale Panichella*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了面向软件工程（SE）任务的能量感知知识蒸馏方法，使用Morph多目标优化蒸馏框架，发现FLOPs并非总能准确反映能耗，通过能量代理模型直接优化CPU/GPU能耗可将推理能耗降低90%、内存使用降低86%，同时仅带来轻微的准确率损失，从而提升LLM在SE应用中的可持续性和可部署性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在SE任务中表现优异，但其高计算需求和能耗引发可持续性担忧，且限制了在消费级硬件和资源受限平台上的部署。现有文献常用FLOPs作为计算成本的衡量指标，但其是否能可靠反映能量消耗尚不明确，需要进一步验证。

Method: 基于Morph（一种多目标优化蒸馏方法）开展受控实验：1）在克隆检测和漏洞预测任务中实证检验FLOPs是否准确反映能耗；2）扩展方法引入能量代理模型，在优化过程中直接估算CPU和GPU能耗；3）将Morph应用于生成式任务（使用CodeT5+进行代码摘要）。

Result: 研究发现：FLOPs并非总能可靠反映能耗；使用能量代理模型能获得更好结果；蒸馏后的学生模型可将推理能耗降低高达90%，内存使用降低86%，仅伴随适度的准确率权衡。

Conclusion: 由直接能量代理而非FLOPs引导的能量感知知识蒸馏，能够显著改善LLM在SE应用中的能耗、可持续性和可部署性，使高效模型能够在消费级硬件上运行。

Abstract: Background: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly being applied to Software Engineering (SE) tasks, achieving high accuracy across problems such as clone detection, vulnerability prediction, and code summarization. However, their high computational demands and energy consumption raise sustainability concerns and hinder their use on consumer hardware and resource-constrained platforms. A common way to report the computational cost of an LLM in the literature and industry is to use the number of Floating Point Operations (FLOPs) required to perform a pass over the network. Aims: This paper investigates the implications of energy-aware knowledge distillation for SE, aiming to improve model efficiency while maintaining performance and to determine whether FLOPs is a reliable energy-aware metric. Method: We conduct a controlled experiment using Morph, a Many-Objective Optimization-based distillation methodology, to empirically examine whether FLOPs accurately reflect energy consumption in Clone Detection and Vulnerability Prediction tasks. We extend this methodology to include energy-surrogate models that directly estimate CPU and GPU energy consumption during optimization, and we apply Morph to generative tasks using CodeT5+ for code summarization. Results: Our results show that FLOPs is not always a reliable indicator of energy consumption, and better results can be achieved by using energy-surrogate models. Distilled student models can reduce inference energy consumption by up to 90\% and memory usage by 86\%, with only modest accuracy trade-offs. Conclusions: Energy-aware knowledge distillation when guided by direct energy surrogates rather than FLOPs can improve the energy consumption, sustainability, and deployability of LLMs for SE applications, enabling efficient models on consumer hardware.

</details>


### [148] [REST API Testing with Verified LLM-Inferred Dependencies and Response-Driven Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17546)
*Tu Nguyen,Thanh Nguyen,Huy Nguyen,Viet Nguyen,Tien N. Nguyen,Vu Nguyen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: APIPilot是一个执行验证驱动的REST API测试框架，它先从OpenAPI规范中利用结构启发式和LLM语义推理推导候选的生产者-消费者依赖关系，再通过实际API执行验证这些依赖假设，最后构建依赖图并通过有界top-k图遍历生成覆盖感知的工作流。通过16个真实REST API服务的评估，APIPilot实现了92.3%的操作覆盖率、最高58.6%的代码覆盖率以及88.1%的工作流执行成功率，并检测到197个独特的5xx错误和规范-执行不一致问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于LLM的REST API测试方法将LLM推断的依赖关系视为正确而不进行执行验证，导致虚假依赖、遗漏可行的操作链以及生成不可行的测试用例，从而影响测试效果。

Method: APIPilot采用三步框架：(1)从OpenAPI规范中利用结构启发式和LLM语义推理推导候选生产者-消费者依赖关系；(2)将依赖视为假设，通过具体API执行进行验证；(3)将已验证的依赖组织成依赖图，通过有界top-k图遍历构建覆盖感知的工作流；此外还包含响应驱动的细化机制，利用运行时响应更新资源池、调整输入生成约束、修剪或修正无效的依赖映射。

Result: 在16个真实REST API服务上，APIPilot实现了92.3%的操作覆盖率、最高58.6%的代码覆盖率和88.1%的工作流执行成功率，优于LLM基础和传统REST API测试基线，并检测到197个独特的5xx失败及规范-执行不匹配问题。

Conclusion: 将依赖推断建立在执行反馈的基础上能显著提升REST API测试的有效性，验证依赖假设和响应驱动细化的结合能有效减少虚假依赖并发现更多真实缺陷。

Abstract: Testing RESTful APIs requires generating sequences of API calls that satisfy dependencies among operations, parameters, and runtime-created resources. Recent LLM-based approaches infer such dependencies and generate test sequences from OpenAPI specifications, but they often treat LLM-inferred relationships as correct without execution-based validation. This can introduce spurious dependencies, miss feasible operation chains, and produce infeasible tests. In this paper, we propose APIPilot}, an execution-validated framework for REST API testing. APIPilot first derives candidate producer-consumer dependencies from OpenAPI specifications using structural heuristics and LLM-based semantic reasoning. It then treats these dependencies as hypotheses and validates them through concrete API executions before using them for test generation. The validated dependencies are organized into a dependency graph from which APIPilot constructs coverage-aware workflows via bounded top-k graph traversal, separating semantic dependency inference from sequence construction. To improve subsequent tests, APIPilot further performs response-driven refinement: runtime responses are analyzed to update resource pools, adjust input-generation constraints, and prune or revise invalid dependency mappings. Empirical evaluation on 16 real-world REST API services shows that APIPilot achieves 92.3% operation coverage, up to 58.6% code coverage, and an 88.1% workflow execution success rate, outperforming both LLM-based and traditional REST API testing baselines. APIPilot also detects 197 unique 5xx failures and specification-execution mismatches, demonstrating the benefit of grounding dependency inference in execution feedback.

</details>


### [149] [Unified Message Model for Heterogeneous Serial Data Exchange Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17642)
*Viktor Sinitsyn,Florian Holzapfel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种统一、与协议无关的串行消息模型，用于显式、确定性地描述串行通信消息。该模型基于数据类型、原子消息元素（容器）和完整消息结构的正式定义，并配套了可配置的消息类型和用户表示方法。该模型已在工业工具环境中实现，可支持机器可读的接口控制文档开发、传输层软件自动生成，以及标准化和弱形式化串行协议的实际工程工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代嵌入式系统日益复杂，集成多种异构设备，涉及从标准化到完全项目自定义的各类串行通信协议。开发效率越来越依赖自动化工具链，而这些工具链需要一个统一、清晰、机器可处理的正式基础。现有方法缺乏对异构串行协议的统一定义模型。

Method: 提出协议无关的串行消息模型，包含三层结构：(1) 数据类型的形式化定义；(2) 原子消息元素（容器）；(3) 完整消息结构。同时引入两种方法：可配置消息类型用于表达结构约束并支持确定性自动化，可配置用户表示用于面向工程化的读写操作。模型在工业工具环境中进行了实现验证。

Result: 实验结果证明该方法能够支持机器可读接口控制文档的开发、传输层软件的自动生成，并能有效处理标准化及弱形式化串行协议的实际工程工作。

Conclusion: 所提出的模型、方法及工具实现为异构串行通信开发中的自动化工具链提供了实用基础，填补了弱形式化串行协议缺乏统一形式化描述的空白。

Abstract: Modern embedded systems are becoming increasingly complex and typically integrate numerous heterogeneous devices, such as controllers, sensors, actuators, and supporting subsystems. As a result, their development and integration involve a wide variety of serial communication protocols, ranging from standardized solutions to partially standardized and fully project-defined formats. Efficient development of such systems increasingly depends on automation toolchains, which in turn require a clear, unified, and machine-processable formal basis. This paper proposes a unified, protocol-agnostic message model for explicit and deterministic description of serial messages. The model is based on formal definition of data types, atomic message elements (containers), and complete message structure. In addition to the model itself, the paper introduces methods for practical work with it, including configurable message types for expressing structural constraints and supporting deterministic automation, as well as configurable user representations for engineering-oriented reading and editing. The proposed model and methods are demonstrated through implementation in an industrial tool environment. The results show that the approach can support machine-readable interface control document development, automated generation of transport-layer software, and practical engineering work with both standardized and weakly formalized serial protocols. Taken together, the proposed model, methods, and tool implementation provide a practical foundation for automation toolchains in heterogeneous serial communication development.

</details>


### [150] [Benchmarking Automated Security Patch Backporting: How Far Are We?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17671)
*Jincheng Yang,Yulong Fu,Chengwei Liu,Lyuye Zhang,Fangyuan Zhang,Bingyang Ren,Yang Liu,Hui Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了Porting Benchmark，一个包含1,234个安全补丁回移植案例的基准数据集，覆盖跨版本、跨分支和跨仓库场景，并配以统一的评估框架。在对五种工具（涵盖程序分析、LLM提示和LLM智能体）进行对齐评估后发现，统一的评估协议改变了工具性能排名，且工具在结构复杂补丁上的性能急剧下降。作者识别了四类根因问题并提出了下一代工具设计方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全补丁回移植工具虽然在各自数据集上报告超过80%的成功率，但评估局限于同质化环境（单一仓库或特定版本），无法反映工具在真实多样化场景中的泛化能力。需要统一的基准和评估框架来客观比较不同范式的工具。

Method: 构建包含1,234个安全补丁回移植案例的Porting Benchmark数据集，覆盖跨版本、跨分支、跨仓库三种场景；设计统一的评估框架，在相同设置下评估五种工具（PortGPT、TSBPort、FixMorph、Mystique等），包含程序分析、LLM提示和LLM智能体三类方法；并对45个动态验证子集进行进一步的可执行验证实验。

Result: 统一评估改变了工具性能格局：PortGPT和TSBPort在复制数据集上保持较强，而FixMorph和Mystique在统一协议下性能大幅下降。在结构复杂补丁上性能急剧下降：最佳commit级成功率从Type-I的85.2%降至Type-IV的24.0%。识别出四类根因问题：目标API意识缺失、跨版本语义不匹配、非局部依赖传播失败、补丁构造或定位失败。动态验证显示静态引用匹配可能高估工具能力，可执行反馈细化在困难案例上提供有限但可测量的改进。

Conclusion: 现有补丁回移植工具的评估存在环境同质化问题，统一基准显示工具的真实泛化能力远低于先前报告。静态评估指标不能完全反映真实修复效果，需要结合动态可执行验证。研究为下一代工具设计提供了具体方向，包括增强目标API意识、处理跨版本语义差异、改善非局部依赖传播和提升补丁构造/定位能力。

Abstract: Automated security patch backporting is critical for mitigating N-day vulnerabilities. Recent tools report success rates above 80% on their respective datasets. However, these evaluations are often confined to homogeneous environments, such as one repository or specific project versions. Consequently, it remains unclear how well these tools generalize beyond their originally targeted scenarios. We present Porting Benchmark, a curated dataset of 1,234 security patch backporting cases spanning cross-version, cross-branch, and cross-repository scenarios, paired with a common evaluation framework. Using this benchmark, we evaluate five tools spanning program analysis, LLM prompting, and LLM agents under aligned settings. Our results show that aligned evaluation changes the apparent performance landscape: PortGPT and TSBPort remain comparatively strong on the Replication Dataset, while FixMorph and Mystique degrade substantially under the common protocol. Performance degrades sharply on structurally complex patches: the best commit-level success rate falls from 85.2% on Type-I patches to 24.0% on Type-IV. We identify four root-cause categories (missing target API awareness, cross-version semantic mismatch, non-local dependency propagation failure, and patch construction or localization failure) and derive concrete directions for next-generation tool design. On a 45-case dynamically validated subset with verified test cases and constructed POCs, we further observe that reference-based benchmark scores do not fully capture real-world remediation: exact match sharply under-credits harder target adaptations, while executable validation reveals residual integration failures in the target that static reference agreement misses. Executable-feedback refinement provides limited but measurable recovery on the hardest executable cases.

</details>


### [151] [GADR: Gathering Architecture Decision Records from Meeting Transcriptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17694)
*Lucas Daniel Costa da Silva,Kiev Gama*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出GADR，一个多智能体自纠正工作流，用于从原始会议转录中提取架构决策并生成Nygard格式的ADR草稿，通过可行性研究验证其在非结构化输入下优于零样本和少样本基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的ADR生成方法假设输入已经合理结构化，但实际中架构决策源于非正式、嘈杂的会议，决策往往是隐式的、碎片化的，并与无关话题交织，这导致单次提示（single-pass prompting）效果退化。

Method: 提出GADR，一个多智能体、自纠正的工作流，从原始会议转录中提取架构决策并生成Nygard格式的ADR草稿。可行性研究包含五个真实项目会议转录、四位资深架构师专家评审和十五名学生评估。

Result: 实验表明GADR能捕获大多数专家识别的决策，生成的草稿被参与者认为清晰有用，在稳定性和结构遵循性上优于零样本和少样本基线。研究还揭示了RAG增强可提升ADR深度但可能引入与转录不一致的风险。

Conclusion: GADR为从非结构化会议转录中自动生成ADR提供了初步证据，同时提出了自动化架构文档中关于可追溯性的开放问题，值得社区关注。

Abstract: Existing LLM-based approaches to Architecture Decision Record (ADR) generation share a critical and largely unexamined assumption: that input is already reasonably structured. In practice, architectural decisions emerge from informal, noisy meetings where choices are implicit, fragmented, and entangled with off-topic dialogue, precisely the conditions under which single-pass prompting degrades. This paper presents GADR, a multi-agent, self-correcting workflow that extracts architectural decisions from raw meeting transcriptions and generates Nygard-formatted ADR drafts. A feasibility study comprising five real project meeting transcripts, expert review by four senior architects, and evaluation by fifteen students provides initial evidence that the agentic workflow captures most expert-identified decisions and produces drafts participants found clear and useful, outperforming zero-shot and few-shot baselines in stability and structural adherence. The study also addresses the underexplored trade-off of RAG-based enrichment improving ADR depth while simultaneously risking transcript-unfaithful content, raising open questions about traceability in automated architectural documentation that we believe is worth the community's attention.

</details>


### [152] [What Aggregate Scores Miss: Measuring Item-Level Regressions in Commercial LLM API Migrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17719)
*Xiaonan Xu,Wenjing Wu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过细粒度的逐项分析揭示了基于聚合基准分数进行大语言模型API迁移决策的局限性。在GPT-5.4到GPT-5.6的升级序列中，作者对900个基准项每项查询50次，发现可靠改进和可靠回归在所有迁移场景中并存，聚合得分掩盖了大量双向的逐项变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商业大语言模型API被厂商弃用时，软件系统必须迁移到后继版本。但迁移决策通常依赖聚合基准分数，这种压缩方法将异质的逐项行为合并为一个净数值，掩盖了重要的细节信息。作者旨在揭示这种压缩所隐藏的信息。

Method: 在三对GPT-5.4到GPT-5.6升级中，对900个公共基准项（研究生知识、奥林匹克数学、指令遵循）每项每模型查询50次。在错误发现率控制和实际显著性阈值下，将每项分类为可靠改进、可靠回归、实际等价或不确定，并对照标签置换零模型进行校准。

Result: 所有九个迁移-基准单元中，可靠改进和可靠回归并存。聚合增益高达7.3个百分点时仍含多达8.3%的可靠回归项；聚合损失时仍含多达10.7%的可靠改进项。指令遵循基准上，严格与宽松评分差距在最新迁移中扩大3.9个百分点，严格评分下3.9点的回归在宽松评分下缩小到0.04点。

Conclusion: 仅基于聚合分数的迁移决策会遗漏大量双向的逐项变化。作者发布了完整响应级存档和逐项评分输出以供进一步研究。

Abstract: Context: Software systems that depend on commercial large language model APIs must migrate to successor versions when vendors deprecate older models. Migration decisions typically rely on aggregate benchmark scores, which compress heterogeneous item-level behaviour into a single net figure. Objective: We measure what that compression conceals. Method: On three pairwise upgrades in the GPT-5.4 to GPT-5.6 Sol product sequence, we query 900 public benchmark items (graduate-level knowledge, olympiad mathematics, instruction following) 50 times per item per model, classify each item as reliably improved, reliably regressed, practically equivalent, or inconclusive under false-discovery-rate control and a practical-significance threshold, and calibrate the results against a label-permutation null. Results: Across all nine migration-benchmark cells, reliable improvements and reliable regressions coexist. Edges with aggregate gains of up to 7.3 percentage points contain up to 8.3% reliably regressed items; edges with aggregate losses contain up to 10.7% reliably improved items. On the instruction-following benchmark, the gap between strict and loose scoring widens by 3.9 percentage points on the latest migration: a 3.9-point regression under strict scoring shrinks to 0.04 points under loose scoring. Conclusion: Migration decisions based on aggregate scores alone miss substantial bidirectional item-level change. The complete response-level archive and per-item scoring outputs are released.

</details>


### [153] [SpecTrum: Specification-Guided Differential Fuzzing for Ethereum Consensus Clients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17738)
*Seokhun Jeong,Gyeongmin Dan,Sukyoung Ryu,Sungjae Hwang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SpecTrum是一个针对以太坊共识算法的测试框架，通过将规范机械化（Consensus-SpecTec）、定义前提覆盖率指标、以及基于规范生成测试输入，来系统性发现共识客户端之间的分歧。在五个主要客户端中发现了27个跨客户端分歧案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以太坊的共识安全依赖于多个独立客户端实现的一致性，但实现错误会导致网络分叉、最终性停滞等严重问题。现有的Python参考实现（consensus-spec）和手工测试套件（spectests）缺乏系统化的方式确保所有有效性条件都被彻底评估，因为规范的有效性是通过运行时行为隐式定义的。

Method: SpecTrum分三个阶段：1) 引入Consensus-SpecTec，将以太坊共识算法的有效性条件显式化为if-前提；2) 定义前提覆盖率（premise coverage）指标，衡量spectests中哪些前提被评估为真/假；3) 开发基于规范的测试生成器，从未被评估为假的前提中提取约束并生成输入来评估它们。

Result: 应用于五个主要以太坊共识客户端，识别出27个跨客户端分歧案例，其中22个无法在没有机械化规范中插入的前提条件下被发现。所有27个案例都能跨分叉版本复现，且将机械化规范扩展到新分叉的工作量与规范差异成正比。

Conclusion: SpecTrum证明了通过机械化规范来显式化有效性条件并系统化测试生成，能够有效发现传统方法无法找到的共识客户端分歧，且该方法具有良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Ethereum's consensus safety relies on independent consensus client implementations agreeing on every state transition. When they diverge due to implementation errors, the network can fork, finality can stall, and severe attacks are possible. To prevent such consensus divergences, Ethereum provides a Python reference implementation (consensus-spec), which acts as a specification, and a hand-crafted official test suite (spectests). However, as an executable implementation, Ethereum's specification defines validity implicitly through runtime behavior. As a result, it lacks a systematic way to ensure that all validity conditions are thoroughly evaluated.
  We present SpecTrum, a framework that addresses this problem in three stages. First, we introduce Consensus-SpecTec, a mechanized specification of the Ethereum consensus algorithm, which makes validity conditions explicit as if-premises. Second, we define premise coverage, a metric that measures which if-premises are evaluated to true and false across spectests. Third, we develop a specification-based test generator that extracts constraints on premises not evaluated to false by spectests and generates inputs to evaluate them. Applying SpecTrum to five major Ethereum consensus clients, we identify 27 cross-client divergence cases, 22 of which cannot be found without the premises inserted in our mechanization. All 27 cases reproduce across fork versions, and extending the mechanized specification to a new fork takes modest effort proportional to the specification difference.

</details>


### [154] [Reshaping the SDLC for Data- and AI-Centric Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17824)
*Mamdouh Alenezi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文探讨数据工程与软件工程实践（通过DataOps、MLOps、LLMOps实现）如何重塑数据密集型及AI赋能系统的软件开发生命周期（SDLC）。研究提出四项贡献：综合文献形成阶段式生命周期转型描述；轻量级形式化系统行为定义；五层自适应生命周期框架；以及概念性研究模型与证据评估，最后提出未来研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SDLC假设系统行为主要由源代码决定，但数据密集型与AI赋能系统的行为由代码、数据和所学模型的交互涌现，且性能会因现实条件偏离训练数据而退化，因此需要重新审视生命周期模型。

Method: 综合跨学科文献（软件工程、数据管理、机器学习系统、人机交互），提出轻量级形式化（以代码、数据、模型配置定义系统行为，需求成为基于评估的规范）及五层框架（工件层、契约层、门控层、控制层、治理层），将维护视为配置漂移下的闭环控制问题。

Result: 转型方向已日益明确，但其幅度尚未被充分量化；作者构建了连接数据工程集成与可测量生命周期结果的概念研究模型，并批判性评估了现有证据基础。

Conclusion: 提出一个以实证为基础的、自适应的、面向数据与AI中心系统的SDLC研究议程。

Abstract: The traditional Software Development Lifecycle (SDLC) assumes that system behavior is determined primarily by source code, allowing correctness to be specified, implemented, and verified through code-centric practices. Data-intensive and AI-enabled systems challenge this assumption because their behavior emerges from the interaction of code, data, and learned models, while performance may degrade as real-world conditions drift from training data. This paper examines how integrating data engineering and software engineering practices, operationalized through DataOps, MLOps, and LLMOps, reshapes the SDLC for these systems. We make four contributions. First, we synthesize literature across software engineering, data management, machine learning systems, and human-centered computing into a phase-structured account of lifecycle transformation spanning requirements, architecture, development, testing, deployment, monitoring, governance, and organization. Second, we provide a lightweight formalization in which system behavior is defined over code, data, and model configurations; requirements become evaluation-led specifications with probabilistic acceptance regions; and promotion is controlled through statistically grounded validation gates. Third, we develop an adaptive five-layer lifecycle framework comprising artifact, contract, gate, control, and governance layers, positioning maintenance as a closed-loop control problem under configuration drift. Fourth, we propose a conceptual research model linking data engineering integration to measurable lifecycle outcomes and critically assess the evidence base. While the direction of transformation is increasingly established, its magnitude remains insufficiently quantified. We conclude with a research agenda for an empirically grounded, adaptive SDLC for data- and AI-centric systems.

</details>


### [155] [What Does It Mean and Why Should I Bother? Motivating Students to Write Better Commit Messages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17993)
*Gergő Balogh,Péter Seres,László Tóth,Attila Szatmári,Szakács Bence,Ádám Zoltán Végh*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过混合方法案例研究验证了软件工程教师对学生提交信息（commit messages）未能发挥应有沟通作用的怀疑，并使用既有质量分类法对学 生与工业项目进行部分复制分析，确认沟通与质量问题在两种情境中普遍存在。基于此，作者设计了WDYM（What Do You Mean?）——一种轻量级、基于角色的教育游戏，旨在在课程约束内揭示并解决提交信息沟通障碍。游戏观察与参与者调查表明，WDYM能有效提升意识并促进反思，但对提交信息写作实践的直接或持续改进证据有限。研究将WDYM定位为针对局部教学问题的有用但不完美的情境化干预手段，未宣称具有普遍适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程教师怀疑学生撰写的提交信息常常无法实现其应有的沟通功能，但该怀疑缺乏实证支持。作者希望通过实证研究验证这一教学直觉，并开发一种能在大学课程约束下运作的干预手段来改善这一现象。

Method: 采用混合方法案例研究：(1) 对来自学生项目和工业案例项目的提交信息进行实证分析，部分复制了已有的提交信息质量分类法；(2) 基于研究发现设计WDYM教育游戏；(3) 通过游戏观察和参与者调查评估WDYM的效果。

Result: 研究确认学生和工业情境中的提交信息均存在反复出现的沟通与质量问题，证实了教师的最初担忧。WDYM在提升意识、促进对提交信息沟通问题的反思方面表现出有效性，但缺乏直接或持续改善提交信息写作实践的充分证据。

Conclusion: WDYM被视为一种有用的、虽不完美且受情境约束的教学干预手段，可应对局部观察到的教学问题。作者明确限定研究结论仅适用于所研究的具体情境，不主张超越该情境的普遍适用性。

Abstract: This paper reports on a locally motivated mixed-methods case study addressing a teaching-related suspicion held by software engineering instructors: that commit messages written by students frequently fail to serve their intended communicative role. To examine this suspicion empirically, we analyzed commit messages from student and industrial case-study projects using a partial replication of an established commit-message quality taxonomy. The results confirm that communication and quality issues occur recurrently in both contexts, substantiating the instructors' initial concern. Motivated by this finding, we devised What Do You Mean? (WDYM), a lightweight, role-based educational game intended to surface and address commit-message communication breakdowns within the constraints of university coursework. Analysis of gameplay observations and participant surveys shows that WDYM is effective in raising awareness and fostering reflection on commit-message communication issues, although it provides limited evidence of immediate or sustained improvement in commit-message writing practices. Taken together, the study presents WDYM as a useful, though imperfect, context-bound intervention for addressing a locally observed instructional problem, without claiming general applicability beyond the studied setting.

</details>


### [156] [The Polyglot's Dilemma: Conformance Testing a Dozen Specs in as Many Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18039)
*A. Jesse Jiryu Davis,Jeremy Mikola,Jeff Yemin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: MongoDB开发了一种基于规范（specification）的测试方法：测试用例用YAML编写一次，由各语言解释器在不同驱动库中执行。经过十一年演变，统一测试格式（Unified Test Format）取代了多种临时格式，删除了超过22,000行测试代码，采用YAML测试的驱动其非一致性缺陷率最多下降86%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MongoDB维护十几个编程语言的客户端库，总计数百万行代码，确保这些库行为一致非常困难但至关重要。由于大多数库是原生实现而非共享核心的包装器，缺乏统一的测试手段来保证跨语言行为一致性。

Method: 采用基于规范（specification）的测试方法：测试用例以YAML格式一次性编写，由各语言特定的解释器在对应驱动库中执行。从多种临时格式逐步演化为统一的测试格式（Unified Test Format），并建立了YAML测试解释器架构。

Result: 统一测试格式使得测试代码减少超过22,000行；采用YAML测试的驱动非一致性（nonconformance）缺陷率最多下降86%（但不同驱动结果有差异）。

Conclusion: 总结了关于声明式测试设计、测试架构、模式演进及统一化局限性的经验教训，表明基于规范的YAML测试方法能有效提升多语言库的行为一致性，但统一化并非没有边界。

Abstract: MongoDB maintains client libraries in a dozen programming languages, used by tens of thousands of organizations and millions of developers. Most are implemented natively rather than as wrappers around a shared core. Ensuring consistent behavior across these libraries, comprising millions of lines of code, is hard but essential. Over eleven years, we developed a specification-based testing approach: tests are written once in YAML and executed by language-specific interpreters for each library. We describe the evolution from many ad-hoc formats to a Unified Test Format, which allowed us to delete over 22,000 lines of test code. The rate of nonconformance bugs fell up to 86% in drivers that adopted YAML tests (though results varied). We report lessons learned about declarative test design, test architecture, schema evolution, and the limits of unification.

</details>


### [157] [The Working Set of a Coding Agent: Coherence Debt in Repository-Scale Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16630)
*Bardia Mohammadi,Lars Klein,Aman Chadha,Akhil Arora,Laurent Bindschaedler*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究将仓库级编码建模为重构耦合事实图，系统性地证实了事实可用性（availability）是决定编码智能体成败的关键因素——不是距离，不是预算，而是事实是否在上下文中。缺失事实导致智能体产生错误工作而非缺席工作，且不同框架为同一事实付出超过十倍的令牌代价差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仓库级编码智能体需要在有限的上下文窗口内保持测试、导入、配置和迁移规则的一致性。作者希望理解：在何种条件下智能体能成功完成任务？事实的可用性（来自上下文或参数记忆）如何决定成败？不同框架为何表现差异巨大？

Method: 将编码任务建模为重构耦合事实图。通过系统性地供应/扣留两个知识通道（上下文、参数记忆），并在七个模型和五个框架（harness）上注入故障（faults），考察事实可用性对任务完成的影响。同时对标准与代码冲突、SWE-bench等场景进行对照实验。

Result: （1）两个通道都空时，任何模型都无法在未见过的API上完成任务；将事实放入提示词可恢复成功。（2）当重命名击败模型对真实库的记忆时，所有七个模型在同一处失败，通过/错过相同的测试。（3）可用性决定结果，距离不起作用：扣留事实精确损失其支持的工作量，供应的事实远离编辑点同样有效。（4）框架间令牌消耗差异超过十倍，但支出更多无法在事实被扣留时恢复任何效果。（5）缺失事实产生错误工作而非缺席——智能体伪造文件或猜测值，基于读取构建的工具探测到的是已被填补的漏洞。（6）模型报告受阻的频率是模型属性，从每次到从不。（7）当标准与代码冲突时，智能体遵循标准，即使其规定更差的代码，过时的约定文件比没有文件代价更高。（8）在SWE-bench上，参数记忆替代了读取，读取不再预测成功。

Conclusion: 框架应确保编辑所依赖的事实对智能体写作时可用，并以智能体产出的内容而非读取行为来检验该可用性。研究的核心贡献是证明了"可用性决定结果"这一原则，并指出当前评估框架的缺陷在于基于读取而非产出来衡量事实可用性。

Abstract: Repository-scale coding requires an agent to keep tests, imports, configuration, and migration rules consistent within a bounded context window. We model this as reconstructing a coupled-fact graph: at each edit, a required fact comes from recent context or parametric memory, and the facts covered by neither form coherence debt. We supply and withhold each channel and inject faults across seven models and five harnesses. As expected, no model completes a task on an unseen API with both channels empty, and putting the facts in the prompt restores success. When a rename defeats what models memorized about a real library, all seven fail in the same place, passing and missing the same tests. Availability decides the outcome and distance does not: withholding a fact costs exactly the work it supports, and a supplied fact works as well far from the edit as next to it. Harnesses pay unequal prices for it: configurations that all pass every test differ more than tenfold in tokens consumed because they rebuild the same content at different rates, and spending more recovers nothing when facts are withheld. A missing fact produces wrong work rather than absent work: an agent asked to act acts, fabricating the file or guessing the value, so instruments built on reads look for a hole already filled. How often it says it is blocked instead is a property of the model, from every trial to none. Availability does not settle every edit: where standard and code disagree, agents follow the standard even when it prescribes the worse code, so a stale convention file costs more than no file. Because parametric memory substitutes for reading, on SWE-bench, where models likely know the repositories, reads no longer predict success. Harnesses should keep the facts an edit depends on available when the agent writes, and check that availability against what the agent produces rather than what it reads.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [158] [GxP-Agent: Process-DAG Topology for Reliable Clinical Trial Programming with LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16890)
*Jaime Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GxP-Agent，一个将临床试验编程流程编码为有向无环图（DAG）的多智能体系统，将数据生成分解为15个领域特定节点。在CDISC-Bench基准上，GxP-Agent配合Claude Sonnet 4.6实现100%结构匹配（49/49变量，254条记录），显著优于最佳检索增强基线（59.2%）和所有单智能体/扁平多智能体方法（0%），并展示了DAG拓扑对弱模型也有提升作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床试验编程（将研究协议转化为符合CDISC标准的分析数据集）是监管提交中的瓶颈，但LLM代码生成在此任务上灾难性失败——五个前沿模型经过11次单次尝试，无一能生成有效的主体级分析数据集。现有方法依赖LLM推理能力本身，缺乏对监管流程顺序的编码，因此需要一种将领域过程知识结构化的解决方案。

Method: GxP-Agent采用多智能体架构，将监管流程顺序编码为DAG，将整体数据集生成任务分解为15个领域特定节点，由携带pharmaverse技能上下文的worker agents执行，并配备验证门控和条件重试机制。论文还构建了CDISC-Bench，一个基于FDA试点提交CDISCPilot01（254名受试者，49个ground-truth ADSL变量）的执行型基准。
