Full recomputation preserves the semantics of mature static solvers, but it repeatedly processes unchanged graph regions when updates are small. Solver-specific dynamic methods can reduce this cost, but their update rules often have limited transferability across objectives, feature representations, and implementations. In addition, localizing computation only by graph distance may omit community context needed by high-quality solvers. We introduce ComNetX, a solver-agnostic hierarchical adaptation framework for local dynamic updates. ComNetX maintains a multi-level community state, expands the updated region, closes it over affected communities, and contracts these communities into compact local instances. This affected-community closure and contraction preserve solver context while restricting computation to the changed part of the graph. The same interface can wrap modularity heuristics, graph-clustering models that use node features, and native dynamic solvers as local backends. We evaluate ComNetX through a multi-backend study on six real networks, longer real-data streams for topology-based backends, and controlled dynamic stochastic block model stress streams. The results show that ComNetX can preserve the quality of strong modularity-based solvers while reducing update time on large graphs: in paired runs on the largest real graph, Local Leiden keeps final modularity within 0.006 of full-snapshot recomputation while achieving a 41.9 +/- 0.2x speedup. The combined protocols also identify regimes where locality breaks down and a full refresh is preferable.

</details>


### [225] [MAG-Bot: A Multi-Agent Auditing Framework for Social Bot Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16908)
*Sichen Zhao,Yalun Qi*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文研究基于大语言模型和社会机器人检测，提出MAG-Bot多智能体框架，将图数据重构为账户级档案进行审计。在TwiBot-22数据集的585账户测试集上，MAG-Bot相比单LLM审计器将准确率从0.5846提升至0.7017，召回率从0.5986提升至0.8289，F1从0.6747提升至0.8028。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于特征的社交机器人检测方法存在局限，而直接使用单个大语言模型进行零样本审计虽可行但存在召回盲区，尤其对稀疏、弱依据账户和角色一致的机器人表现不佳，需要更有效的证据分解与盲区修正机制。

Method: 提出MAG-Bot，一个基于LangGraph的多智能体系统。将TwiBot-22的图数据重构为账户级档案，融合档案元数据、行为统计、情境线索和最近推文。采用角色约束的多智能体分解方法，即诊断驱动的行为与情境专家强化机制，而非简单的聚合技巧或事后辩论。

Result: 零样本单LLM审计可行但存在召回盲区；MAG-Bot相比单LLM在585账户测试集上准确率从0.5846提升到0.7017，召回率从0.5986提升到0.8289，F1从0.6747提升到0.8028；增益主要来自诊断驱动的行为与情境专家强化，而非聚合或辩论技巧。

Conclusion: 多智能体LLM审计的核心价值在于角色约束的证据分解和盲区修正，诊断驱动的专家强化是提升性能的关键，为社交机器人检测提供了新的范式。

Abstract: This paper studies social bot detection as dossier-based account auditing with large language models and a graph-structured multi-agent framework. From TwiBot-22, we reconstruct graph data into account-level records combining profile metadata, behavioral statistics, contextual cues, and recent tweets. We compare conventional feature-based baselines, a direct zero-shot Single-LLM auditor, and MAG-Bot, a LangGraph-based multi-agent system. Three findings emerge. First, zero-shot Single-LLM auditing is feasible but has recall-related blind spots, especially on sparse, weakly grounded accounts and coherent role-bound personas. Second, role-constrained multi-agent decomposition substantially improves over Single-LLM: on the 585-account test split, MAG-Bot improves accuracy from 0.5846 to 0.7017, recall from 0.5986 to 0.8289, and F1 from 0.6747 to 0.8028. Third, the gain comes mainly from diagnosis-driven strengthening of the behavioral and contextual specialists, not aggregation tricks or post-hoc debate. Multi-agent LLM auditing therefore derives its main value from role-constrained evidence decomposition and blind-spot correction.

</details>


### [226] [Network Denoising Revisited: A Ricci-Flow-Inspired Graph Diffusion Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16923)
*Ye Fang,Chuan-Xian Ren*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Ricci-Diffusion，一种受 Ricci flow 启发的曲率引导图扩散去噪方法。该方法通过边级曲率调节扩散核中的局部信息传输，并引导边权重更新以修正图的几何结构，在理论分析和实验上均优于传统相似度驱动的扩散方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界的网络常因测量误差和内在随机性而受到噪声污染，阻碍了有意义结构的发现。现有去噪方法大多依赖基于相似度的扩散，忽略了图的非欧几里得几何特性——局部变化会导致异构的信息传输，因此需要从几何角度重新审视网络去噪问题。

Method: 提出 Ricci-Diffusion 方法：(1) 受 Ricci flow 启发，扩散过程展现出类似 Ricci flow 的演化；(2) 相对边级曲率调制扩散核中的局部传输；(3) 曲率引导边权重更新，使图几何趋于更规则化；(4) 提供理论分析证明曲率能区分相似度扩散核无法区分的图结构，并能在一阶扩散更新中引入修正项。

Result: 在真实世界和合成图上的实验表明，曲率引导的更新和曲率均匀化能改善结构恢复效果和下游任务表现。理论分析证明扩散过程收敛到稳定的去噪网络，且能显式刻画局部几何间的传输异质性。

Conclusion: 曲率引导的扩散方法能有效地对含噪网络进行去噪，相比传统的相似度驱动扩散方法，能更好地恢复图结构和提升下游性能，为网络去噪提供了新的几何视角。

Abstract: Networks provide a fundamental representation of relationships among entities. However, real-world networks are often corrupted by noise caused by measurement errors and inherent stochasticity, hindering the discovery of meaningful structure. Most denoising methods rely on similarity-driven diffusion and ignore the non-Euclidean geometry of graphs, where local variations induce heterogeneous information transport. This motivates a geometric revisit of network denoising. In this work, we propose Ricci-Diffusion, a curvature-guided graph diffusion method inspired by Ricci flow. Specifically, Ricci-Diffusion exhibits a Ricci-flow-like evolution, in which relative edge-level curvature modulates local transport in the diffusion kernel and guides edge-weight updates toward a more regular graph geometry. We further provide a theoretical analysis showing that curvature can distinguish graph structures that common similarity-driven diffusion kernels fail to separate, and that curvature induces first-order corrections in one-step diffusion updates. The resulting diffusion process explicitly characterizes transport heterogeneity across local geometries and admits theoretical convergence to a stable denoised network. Results on real-world and synthetic graphs show that curvature-guided updates and curvature homogenization improve structure recovery and downstream performance.

</details>


### [227] [Structure, Topics, and Diffusion Effects of Bluesky Starter Packs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17489)
*Andrea Failla,Vander Freitas,Giulio Rossetti,Carlos Ferreira*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文对Bluesky平台上超过5万个英文starter packs和60多万相关用户进行了大规模实证分析，研究了其结构组织、主题构成以及对内容传播的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线社交平台中新用户发现（user discovery）是一个核心挑战，尤其是在用户入驻阶段。Bluesky推出starter packs作为引导用户建立社交网络的机制，但缺乏对其结构、组成和影响的系统性理解。

Method: 对50,000多个英文starter packs和600,000多个相关用户进行大规模实证分析，包括：1) 通过结构分析刻画packs之间的重叠与互联关系；2) 通过主题建模分析其内容构成；3) 通过匹配的事件研究（matched event-study）分析评估加入starter pack对内容扩散（repost活动）的影响。

Result: 研究发现：1) Starter packs形成了一个高度互联的生态系统，pack之间重叠显著，反映预先存在的社区；2) 主题建模显示主题分布呈倾斜格局，自动生成的个人packs占主导，另有若干主题社区，这些主题社区结构属性相似但采用模式差异明显；3) 匹配事件研究表明，被纳入starter pack与短期转发活动的显著增加密切相关。

Conclusion: Starter packs在塑造新用户的社交网络构建和内容传播方面具有重要作用，但生态系统存在不均衡性（自动生成packs主导），其影响主要体现在短期内容扩散上。

Abstract: User discovery is a central challenge in online social platforms, particularly during onboarding. Bluesky, a decentralized microblogging platform built on the AT Protocol, introduced starter packs: curated collections of accounts that users can follow in a single action to bootstrap their social network. In this paper, we present a large-scale empirical analysis of more than 50,000 English-language starter packs and over 600,000 associated users. We characterize their structural organization, topical composition, and impact on content diffusion. Our results show that starter packs form a highly interconnected ecosystem with substantial overlap across packs that largely reflects pre-existing communities. Topic modeling reveals a skewed landscape dominated by automatically generated personal packs alongside several thematic communities, which exhibit similar structural properties but markedly different adoption patterns. Finally, a matched event-study analysis shows that inclusion in a starter pack is strongly associated with a substantial increase in short-term repost activity.

</details>


### [228] [The Brazilian Vaccination Debate on YouTube: Topics, Perspectives, and Engagement Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17502)
*Matheus S. Azevedo,Geovana S. de Oliveira,Andrea Failla,Alexandre M. de Sousa,Fabricio Murai,Ana Paula C. da Silva,Carlos H. G. Ferreira*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本研究通过多层级计算文本分析，对2018至2024年间巴西YouTube上127万条疫苗相关评论进行分析，整合语义、立场、参与度和时间维度，揭示了受众如何重构生产者框架的健康内容，以及疫苗争议在疫情高峰期后的持续存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究往往将语义结构、立场、错误信息和参与度割裂开来研究，缺乏对这些维度随时间变化的交互作用的深入理解，尤其是在疫苗争议这一复杂公共健康传播议题上。

Method: 基于语言模型的多层级计算文本分析，对127万条巴西YouTube评论（2018-2024）进行分析，整合主题提取、参与度元数据、对话时序、基于立场的疫苗态度，以及疫情前、疫情中、疫情后三个时期，并对比标题（生产者框架）与评论（受众话语）之间的差异。

Result: COVID-19主导了标题中的生物医学和信息主题，而评论则涵盖个人健康报告、疫苗效果、信息可信度、阴谋叙事和政治争议。健康相关宏观主题在规模和持续性上占主导，而阴谋和政治主题则与更快的互动和更集中的反对疫苗参与相关。疫情后的活动仍集中在健康体验、疫苗效果和信息可信度上，表明疫情前主题配置未恢复。

Conclusion: 通过整合语义、互动、立场和时间维度，本研究揭示了受众如何重构生产者框架的健康内容，并表明疫苗争议在急性疫情期之后仍然持续存在。

Abstract: Vaccination debates are central to online public health communication, as COVID-19 intensified disputes over scientific authority, institutional trust, and political identity. Yet studies often isolate semantic structure, stance, misinformation, and engagement, leaving their interplay over time poorly understood. We conduct a multilevel computational text analysis based on language models applied to 1.27 million Brazilian YouTube comments from 2018 to 2024, using what is, to our knowledge, the largest dataset of Brazilian vaccine discourse on the Web. We contrast producer framing in titles with audience discourse in comments, integrating Topic-derived themes with engagement metadata, conversational timing, stance-derived vaccine positions, and pre-pandemic, pandemic, and post-pandemic periods. Results show that COVID-19 dominates biomedical and informational themes in titles, whereas comments span personal health reports, vaccine effects, information credibility, conspiracy narratives, and political disputes. Health-related macro-topics dominate in scale and persistence, while conspiratorial and political themes are associated with faster interactions and a greater concentration of vaccine-opposing engagement. Post-pandemic activity remains centered on health experiences, vaccine effects, and information credibility, indicating no return to the pre-pandemic thematic configuration. By integrating semantic, interactional, stance, and temporal dimensions, this study shows how audiences reframe producer-framed health content and how vaccine controversies persist beyond the acute pandemic period.

</details>


### [229] [Against Political Polarization: A Unified Framework for Tracing Evolving Political Ideologies on Social Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17987)
*Yijie Xu,Chao Wang,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 提出TSN4PI框架，用于追踪社交媒体上政治意识形态的演化。包含PIDN（利用大语言模型+风格迁移+无监督域适应检测意识形态并过滤噪声）和PIPN（利用时序图神经网络预测意识形态转变）两个模块，并发布两个大规模数据集，在X和Truth Social平台上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体对政治话语影响巨大，但理解个人政治意识形态及其时间动态面临多重挑战：数据稀缺、大量非政治内容干扰、人工标注代价高且易产生偏差，以及难以建模未来意识形态倾向。

Method: 提出TSN4PI统一框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) PIDN（政治意识形态检测网络）利用大语言模型结合风格迁移和无监督域适应，实现鲁棒的意识形态检测并过滤跨域噪声数据中的无关内容；(2) PIPN（政治意识形态预测网络）采用时序图神经网络预测未来的意识形态转变。

Result: 在X和Truth Social等多个平台上进行了大量案例研究，验证了TSN4PI的有效性，并提供了关于政治极化和在线意识形态演化的实证见解。同时发布了两个大规模数据集供非商业研究使用。

Conclusion: TSN4PI在方法论和实证层面均推动了该领域发展，为理解政治极化与在线意识形态演化提供了细致入微的视角，兼顾了意识形态的存在性、强度与演化分析。

Abstract: The rapid growth of social media has greatly influenced political discourse, highlighting the need to understand individual political ideologies and their temporal dynamics. This task faces challenges such as data scarcity, abundant non-political content, costly and bias-prone manual annotation, and difficulty in modeling future ideological inclinations. To address these issues, we propose TSN4PI, a unified framework for tracking the evolution of political ideologies on social media. It includes two core modules. The PIDN uses large language models with style transfer and unsupervised domain adaptation to enable robust ideology detection and filter irrelevant content from noisy, cross-domain data. The PIPN employs temporal graph neural networks to predict future ideological shifts, enabling comprehensive analysis of ideology presence, intensity, and evolution. We release two large-scale datasets for noncommercial research use to facilitate further work. Extensive case studies on multiple platforms (X and Truth Social) validate the effectiveness of TSN4PI and provide empirical insights into political polarization and the evolution of online ideologies. Our findings offer a nuanced perspective, advancing both methodological development and empirical understanding in this field.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [230] [An O-RAN-Assisted MARL Approach for Dynamic Sidelink and Infrastructure Selection in V2X Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17210)
*Maria Katarine Santana Barbosa,Kelvin Lopes Dias*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Open RAN的多智能体强化学习（MARL）资源感知系统，用于6G车联网（IoV）中的V2X通信管理。系统通过聚类策略让每个智能体管理一组V2X对，采用集中训练分散执行的架构，在侧行链路（SL）和网络传输之间智能选择以缓解干扰、优化资源利用并提升服务质量。实验表明该方法相比单智能体方案在车辆场景下降低21%损失和19%延迟，在VRU共存场景下降低18%损失和30%延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G车联网中基于侧行链路的V2X直连通信会显著增加车辆间及车辆与智能交通系统其他实体间的干扰，导致V2V通信和弱势道路使用者（VRU）上行资源退化甚至饥饿。现有解决方案主要聚焦于资源分配和配对选择，缺乏对通信模式和整个网络的综合管理方法。

Method: 论文利用Open RAN管理V2X通信，利用其网络全局视图的控制循环和开放接口框架为机器学习资源决策提供支持。提出多智能体强化学习（MARL）资源感知系统，采用聚类策略使每个智能体管理一组通信对而非单个对，并采用集中训练分散执行（CTDE）架构。策略使用离线训练和离策略方法，每个智能体存储经验用于微调，在侧行链路和网络传输之间最优选择。

Result: 在仅车辆场景中，MARL方法相比单智能体方法降低平均损失21%、延迟19%；在VRU共存场景中，损失降低18%、延迟降低30%。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于Open RAN的MARL资源感知系统能够有效缓解侧行链路通信干扰，优化资源利用率并提升服务质量。聚类策略在降低系统复杂度的同时保持了良好的性能表现，验证了集中训练分散执行架构在V2X场景中的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: Future applications in the 6G-based Internet of Vehicles will leverage sidelink (SL) transmissions in Vehicle-to-Everything (V2X) scenarios. However, SL-based direct communication can significantly increase interference among vehicles and between vehicles and other entities of the Intelligent Transportation System. Thus, both Vehicle-to-Vehicle communications and Vulnerable Road Users (VRUs) uplink resources may be degraded or subject to starvation. Existing solutions primarily focus on improving resource allocation and pair selection. Nonetheless, they lack a comprehensive approach to tackle the communication modes and the entire network. To address these challenges, this paper leverages Open RAN to manage V2X communication and proposes a multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) resource-aware system. Open RAN provides control loops through a global view of the network and also an open interface-based framework for machine learning models applied to resource decision-making. Meanwhile, the MARL model aims to mitigate interference, optimize resource usage, and enhance quality of service by optimally selecting between sidelink and network transmissions. To reduce system complexity, this work employs a clustering strategy. Each agent manages a group of pairs, rather than assigning one agent to each pair. The solution supports this design by adopting a centralized training with decentralized execution approach, empowered by Open RAN. The strategy uses offline training and an off-policy approach, in which each agent stores experience for fine-tuning. Results indicate that the MARL approach reduces average loss by 21% and latency by 19% in Vehicle-only scenarios. In coexistence VRU scenarios, loss and latency drop by 18% and 30%, respectively, compared to the single-agent approach.

</details>


### [231] [LoRIS: LoRaWAN-based IoT Platform for Sustainability Monitoring in Hotels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17467)
*Yash Pandey,Angus Gray,Reza Serati,Oscar Zhu,Emil Juvan,Anna Zinn,Danyelle Greene,Qingqing Chen,Sarah MacInnes,Siamak Layeghy,Sara Dolnicar,Marius Portmann*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了LoRIS，一个基于LoRaWAN的酒店可持续性监测物联网平台，自2022年2月起运行，覆盖澳大利亚和斯洛文尼亚的21个站点，部署了850个传感器，已生成超过2.02亿条传感器记录，每日处理约24.5万条上行消息，并成功支撑了7项现场研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 酒店行业是全球温室气体排放、水资源压力和废弃物产生的重要来源，但酒店可持续性报告受限于粗粒度、人工收集的运营数据，缺乏高分辨率的资源消耗监测手段。

Method: 设计并部署了遵循LoRaWAN参考模型的物联网平台，涵盖资源消耗、环境条件和客人行为的测量；采用隐私优先设计指导传感器类型选择和部署分区，通过端到端加密保护从传感器到仪表盘的数据；架构针对酒店运营现实（严格的IT政策、客人隐私期望、快速可逆安装、多年电池寿命）进行了优化，运行在无服务器基础设施上。

Result: 系统自2022年2月起运行，覆盖21个站点（澳洲AU915和斯洛文尼亚EU868两个监管区域），部署850个传感器、19种类型，产生超2.02亿条记录，每日接收约24.5万条上行消息；成功用于7项涉及食物浪费、能源和水消耗的现场研究，包括同时测量环境结果和客人满意度的对照干预实验。

Conclusion: 研究表明LoRaWAN传感技术可以在运营中的酒店大规模部署，且不会损害客人体验或隐私，证明了该方案在真实酒店环境中的可行性和可扩展性。

Abstract: The hospitality sector is a major source of global greenhouse gas emissions, water stress, and waste generation, yet sustainability reporting in hotels remains constrained by coarse, manually collected operational data. We present LoRIS (LoRaWAN-based IoT platform for sustainability monitoring in hotels), a LoRaWAN-based sensing system that delivers high-resolution measurements of resource consumption, environmental conditions, and guest behaviour across geographically distributed hotel properties. The architecture follows the canonical LoRaWAN reference model and is built for the operational realities of hospitality deployments: restrictive hotel IT policies, guest privacy expectations, rapid and reversible installation, and multi-year battery operation. Privacy-by-design guides modality selection and deployment zoning, and end-to-end encryption protects data from sensor to dashboard. This system has been running since February 2022 and currently spans 850 sensors of 19 types across 21 sites in Australia and Slovenia, covering both the AU915 and EU868 regulatory regions. The platform has generated over 202 million sensor records and ingests approximately 245,000 uplink messages per day on managed serverless infrastructure. Our system has been successfully used for seven field studies spanning food waste, energy consumption, and water consumption, including controlled intervention experiments that measure environmental outcomes and guest satisfaction in parallel. This system shows that LoRaWAN sensing can be deployed at scale in operational hotels without compromising guest experience or privacy.

</details>


### [232] [Predict Before Replay: Joint FEC and Flight Control for Reliable Scale-Up Links](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17503)
*Fan Yang,Jiaqi Liu,Tao Jiang,Zhan Wang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: PREFACE是一个针对加速器互联网络中时间相关突发错误的前FEC控制器。它通过两状态贝叶斯滤波器将纠错符号观测转化为下一数据块突发后验概率，并联合选择FEC强度与未确认数据上限，在ns-3模拟器中基于UALink 200G 1.0重放语义实现，可将吞吐量提升10.52%，P99延迟降低50.75%，重放减少47.52%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加速器互联fabric在数百Gbps串行链路上传输延迟敏感数据块，其可靠性流水线先依赖FEC，再检测残留故障并重放未确认数据。在高线速率下，延迟反馈使得后续数据块在残留故障报告前就进入重放窗口，导致一个损坏数据块的失败可能放大为整个后缀的重新传输，造成严重的性能损失。

Method: 提出PREFACE，一个前FEC控制器：(1) 使用两状态贝叶斯滤波器将纠错符号观测量化为下一数据块的突发后验概率；(2) 联合选择FEC强度与未确认数据块上限，动态调整编码策略以适应时间相关的突发错误模式。

Result: 在ns-3中基于UALink 200G 1.0重放语义实现PREFACE，取得以下改进：吞吐量提升10.52%，P99延迟降低50.75%，重放减少47.52%，并在建模的环形AllReduce场景中提升13.1%-27.0%。

Conclusion: PREFACE通过主动预测突发错误并动态调整FEC策略，有效缓解了时间相关突发错误在高速互联中的重放放大问题，显著提升了可靠性和性能，特别对延迟敏感的加速器互联工作负载（如AllReduce）有显著收益。

Abstract: Scale-up accelerator fabrics send latency-sensitive flits over serial links at hundreds of gigabits per second. Their reliability pipeline first relies on FEC, then detects residual failures and replays unacknowledged data. At these line rates, delayed feedback lets later flits enter the replay window before a residual failure is reported, so standard replay can amplify one corrupted flit into a suffix retransmission. This paper presents PREFACE, a pre-FEC controller for temporally correlated burst errors. A two-state Bayesian filter converts corrected-symbol observations into a next-flit burst posterior and jointly selects FEC strength with an outstanding-flit cap. We implement PREFACE in ns-3 with publicly verifiable UALink 200G 1.0 replay semantics. PREFACE improves goodput by 10.52%, lowers P99 latency by 50.75%, cuts replay by 47.52%, and improves modeled ring AllReduce by 13.1--27.0%.

</details>


### [233] [Completion-Path Credits: Multi-Resource Control for Scale-Up Fabrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17523)
*Fan Yang,Jiaqi Liu,Tao Jiang,Zhan Wang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: SemaCredit是一种接收端控制器，通过按目标资源需求向量（HBM、Atomic、响应）准入远程内存操作，并在各资源阶段完成时逐个归还配额，以替代传统的按字节计量的信用机制。在确定性事件模拟器中，相比字节级基线，该方法在Atomic争用和响应incast下分别将小操作P99延迟降低52.4%和10.2%，同时在HBM热点流量上保持同等性能，并在应用混合流量中带来显著延迟改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的按字节计量的信用机制（byte-denominated credits）虽然能有效保护链路缓冲区和流式HBM流量，但对于以Atomic执行或响应注入为主导的小型操作，其资源占用特征与字节数不成比例，导致资源计量不准确，无法精确反映目标端接收资源的真实需求。

Method: 提出SemaCredit接收端控制器：为每个远程内存操作维护一个目标资源需求向量，分别对应HBM、Atomic执行引擎和响应引擎三类资源；采用多信号量机制，当操作的各资源阶段（HBM访问、Atomic执行、响应注入）完成时，相应归还其占用的资源配额，从而实现对不同类型资源需求的精细管控。

Result: 在包含多路径队列、八个HBM分区、串行Atomic引擎和响应引擎的确定性事件模拟器中：1) HBM热点流量下与强按资源字节基线持平；2) Atomic争用下小操作P99延迟降低52.4%；3) 响应incast下P99延迟降低10.2%；4) AllReduce形状和远程读形状流量分别获得57.7%和14.5%的P99延迟改进；5) HBM主导的MoE流量下与字节信用持平。

Conclusion: SemaCredit通过基于资源需求向量的多信号量准入控制，能够更准确地在共享接收端资源上调度异构远程内存操作，在保持HBM主导流量性能不变的前提下，显著降低小操作和高争用场景下的尾延迟，展示了按资源类型而非仅按字节计量流量的优势。

Abstract: Scale-up fabrics connecting GPUs and AI accelerators carry tensor transfers together with remote reads, writes, atomics, and notifications over shared target-side receiver resources. Byte-denominated credits protect link buffers and streaming HBM traffic, but poorly represent small operations dominated by Atomic execution or response injection. This paper presents SemaCredit, a receiver controller that admits each remote-memory operation against a vector of target-resource demands and returns each component when its corresponding HBM, Atomic, or response stage completes. In a deterministic event simulator with multipath queues, eight HBM partitions, a serialized Atomic engine, and a response engine, SemaCredit matches a strong per-resource byte baseline on HBM-hotspot traffic while reducing small-operation P99 latency by 52.4% under Atomic contention and 10.2% under response incast. Application-shaped mixes show 57.7% and 14.5% P99 latency improvements for AllReduce-shaped and remote-read-shaped traffic while matching byte credits on HBM-dominated MoE traffic.

</details>


### [234] [Array-Based Molecular Pulse Encoding for Neuro-Spike Communication in Intra-Body Nano-networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17675)
*Keyvan Aghababaiyan*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于神经脉冲阵列的通信方案，利用不同分子脉冲的排列顺序编码信息，消除对精确时间同步的依赖，相比已有符号同步模型可提升75%-150%的通信速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经脉冲通信传统上依赖瞬时脉冲率和时间间隔来传递信息，但这些时间编码方案要求收发双方精确时间同步，这对资源受限的纳米机器而言是巨大挑战。因此需要开发在降低阶次同步（如符号同步）条件下仍能有效工作的通信方案。

Method: 提出一种新颖的神经脉冲阵列通信方案，通过纳米机器发出的不同分子脉冲的特定排列顺序来编码信息。由于基于发射序列而非精确时序来区分符号，因此消除了对严格时间同步的需求。理论上推导了符号间干扰（ISI）概率、错误概率和可达通信速率的表达式。

Result: 分析和数值结果表明，所提出的阵列方案显著优于已有符号同步模型，在不同扩散系数下通信速率提升达75%-150%。

Conclusion: 基于阵列的通信方案通过利用分子脉冲的空间/序列排列信息，有效规避了时间同步难题，为体内部署纳米网络的可靠通信提供了可行且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate a neuro-spike communication system designed to bridge severed connections between damaged neurons using auxiliary nano-machines. Natural neuro-spike communication typically relies on instantaneous spike rates and temporal intervals to convey information. However, these temporal encoding schemes require exact time synchronization between the transmitter and receiver, a requirement that poses a significant challenge for resource-constrained nano-machines. To address this issue, it is imperative for future intra-body nano-networks to develop communication schemes that operate under reduced-order synchronization (e.g., symbol-synchronized). In this paper, we propose a novel neuro-spike array-based communication scheme where information is encoded through the specific arrangement of distinct molecular pulses emitted by nano-machines. By distinguishing symbols based on the sequence of these emissions rather than their exact timing, the need for stringent time synchronization is eliminated. We theoretically analyze the performance of the proposed scheme by deriving expressions for the probability of inter-symbol interference (ISI), error probability, and the achievable communication rate. Analytical and numerical results demonstrate that our array-based scheme significantly outperforms previously proposed symbol-synchronized models, providing a 75% - 150% enhancement in the communication rate across various diffusion coefficients.

</details>


### [235] [Threat Aware Task Offloading and Caching for Secure UAV Assisted Vehicular Consumer Electronics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17794)
*Xiaoteng Yang,Sunil Prajapat,Zheng Lin*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无人机辅助的协作式车载边缘计算（VEC）架构，结合威胁感知的任务卸载与智能时空缓存策略，并设计TAGO框架（PPO与Frank-Wolfe算法结合）以最小化端到端任务延迟并提升缓存利用率，仿真验证了其在时延和缓存效率上的显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车载消费电子服务对计算密集型和延迟敏感型任务要求越来越高，对VEC系统的效率、可靠性和安全性提出严格要求。在动态车载环境中，基于推理的信息泄露和异常通信行为严重威胁系统性能和数据隐私，亟需同时考虑安全感知与缓存优化的解决方案。

Method: 1) 提出无人机辅助的协作式VEC架构，集成RSU和无人机边缘节点的智能时空缓存；2) 建立安全感知的上行传输模型，捕捉信息泄露风险和异常通信模式；3) 构建联合优化问题，最小化端到端任务延迟同时提升缓存利用率；4) 设计TAGO框架：采用近端策略优化（PPO）进行自适应任务卸载，利用基于Frank-Wolfe算法的梯度缓存更新捕获时空服务流行度。

Result: 仿真结果表明，与多种基线策略相比，所提方法显著降低了任务延迟并提升了缓存效率，验证了其在安全高效无人机辅助车载消费电子系统中的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的威胁感知联合优化框架能够有效兼顾VEC系统的安全性、时效性和缓存资源利用率，为动态车载环境下的无人机辅助边缘计算提供了可行且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Vehicular consumer electronics increasingly support computation-intensive and latency-sensitive services, imposing stringent efficiency, reliability, and security requirements on vehicular edge computing (VEC) systems. In dynamic vehicular environments, inference-based information leakage and anomalous communication behaviors further threaten system performance and data privacy. To address these challenges, this paper proposes a UAV-assisted cooperative VEC architecture that integrates threat-aware task offloading with intelligent spatiotemporal caching across roadside units (RSUs) and UAV edge nodes. A security-aware uplink transmission model is developed to capture potential information leakage risks and abnormal communication patterns, enabling adaptive offloading decisions. We formulate a joint optimization problem to minimize end-to-end task execution delay while improving cache utilization under limited computing and storage resources. To efficiently solve this problem, a Threat-Aware Joint Optimization (TAGO) framework is designed by combining proximal policy optimization for adaptive task offloading and a gradient-based caching update derived from the Frank-Wolfe algorithm to capture spatiotemporal service popularity. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed approach significantly reduces task delay and improves cache efficiency compared with several baseline strategies, showing its effectiveness for secure and efficient UAV-assisted vehicular consumer electronics systems.

</details>
